## 6. Sessions (active-session presence) — `active-sessions`

**Path / glob:** root `sessions`, include `*.json` → `~/.claude/sessions/{pid}.json`
- `ACTIVE_SESSION_STREAM = "active-sessions"` (`adapter.rs:117`), `ACTIVE_SESSION_DECODER = "claude-active-session"` (`adapter.rs:133`)
- Driver is **`DriverSpec::Presence(PresenceObjectConfig { include_content: true, max_content_bytes: ACTIVE_SESSION_MAX_BYTES })`**, `ACTIVE_SESSION_MAX_BYTES = 64 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:159`; spec `adapter.rs:424-443`)
- Entity scope `Custom("process_presence")`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`
- Locator `{observed-pid}.json`, identity inputs `["observed-pid","native-session-id"]` (`adapter.rs:3888-3896`)

**Filename → ID** (`ClaudeActiveSessionContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1178-1202`): exactly 1 component; strip `.json`; stem must be all ASCII digits; parse as `u32`; must be `> 0`.

```rust
struct ClaudeActiveSessionContext { native_pid: u32 }
```

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeActiveSessionDocument {
    pub(super) pid: u32,
    pub(super) session_id: String,
    pub(super) cwd: String,
    pub(super) started_at: i64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) kind: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) entrypoint: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) name: Option<String>,
    /// Deliberately decoded but not projected: observed values look like epoch
    /// milliseconds, but native transition semantics are not fixture-proven.
    #[serde(default, rename = "nameSince")]
    pub(super) _name_since: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) status: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) updated_at: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) status_updated_at: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) proc_start: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) version: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) peer_protocol: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) name_source: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) bridge_session_id: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) messaging_socket_path: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:84-119`)

**JSON keys:** `pid` (u32, required), `sessionId` (required non-empty), `cwd` (required non-empty), `startedAt` (i64 epoch-ms, required, ≥ 0), `kind`, `entrypoint`, `name`, `nameSince` (**decoded and discarded** — `_name_since`), `status`, `updatedAt` (Option<i64>, ≥ 0), `statusUpdatedAt` (Option<i64>, ≥ 0), `procStart` (Option<String>), `version`, `peerProtocol` (Option<u32>), `nameSource`, `bridgeSessionId`, `messagingSocketPath`.

**Decode** (`decode_active_session`, `sidecars.rs:532-632`):
- `record.state == SourceRecordState::Absent` → `IgnoredKnown` (process exited)
- `pid == 0 || pid != context.native_pid` → contract loss ("payload pid does not match the source file name")
- any negative epoch-ms → contract loss
- Presence identity key: `[pid_be_bytes, session_id]` then either `["proc-start", procStart]` when present, or `["session-start", startedAt_be_bytes]` (`sidecars.rs:578-590`) — so PID reuse with a different process start makes a new presence entity
- Emits `Fact::Presence(PresenceFact { .. })` plus `session` and `run` entity keys derived from `sessionId`

```rust
pub struct PresenceFact {
    pub presence: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub run: EntityKey,
    pub native_session_id: String,
    pub native_pid: u32,
    pub cwd: String,
    pub started_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub native_kind: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub native_status: Option<String>,
    pub updated_at: Option<QualifiedTimestamp>,
    pub status_updated_at: Option<QualifiedTimestamp>,
    pub native_process_started_at: Option<String>,
    pub version: Option<String>,
    pub peer_protocol: Option<u32>,
    pub name_source: Option<String>,
    pub bridge_session_id: Option<String>,
    pub messaging_socket_path: Option<String>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2150-2171`)

**Also read by the support probe** (`support_probe.rs:32-66`): `sessions/*.json` with an all-digit stem, bounded to 256 entries × 64 KiB; extracts only the `version` string (1..=256 bytes) as the `active-session.version` marker; more than one distinct version across roots sets `contradictory_markers`.

## 7. `projects/*/sessions-index.json` — `session-indexes`

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/sessions-index.json` → `~/.claude/projects/{projectSlug}/sessions-index.json`
- `SESSION_INDEX_STREAM = "session-indexes"` (`adapter.rs:124`), `SESSION_INDEX_DECODER = "claude-session-index"` (`adapter.rs:140`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `SESSION_INDEX_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:165`; spec `adapter.rs:556-574`)
- Entity scope `Project`, authority **`Supplemental`**, `Interactive`

**Filename → ID** (`ClaudeSessionIndexContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1374-1389`): exactly 2 components, `components[0]` non-empty, `components[1] == "sessions-index.json"` → `project_slug`.

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeSessionIndexDocument {
    pub(super) version: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) original_path: Option<String>,
    pub(super) entries: Vec<ClaudeSessionIndexEntryDocument>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeSessionIndexEntryDocument {
    pub(super) session_id: String,
    pub(super) full_path: String,
    pub(super) file_mtime: u64,
    pub(super) first_prompt: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) summary: Option<String>,
    pub(super) message_count: u64,
    pub(super) created: String,
    pub(super) modified: String,
    pub(super) git_branch: String,
    pub(super) project_path: String,
    pub(super) is_sidechain: bool,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:218-242`)

**JSON keys:** top level `version` (u64), `originalPath` (Option<String>), `entries[]`. Per entry: `sessionId`, `fullPath`, `fileMtime` (u64 ms), `firstPrompt`, `summary` (Option), `messageCount` (u64), `created`, `modified`, `gitBranch`, `projectPath`, `isSidechain` (bool). All non-`summary` entry fields are **required** (no `#[serde(default)]`).

**Decode** (`decode_session_index`, `sidecars.rs:1221-1343`) — contract-loss gates:
- absent record → `IgnoredKnown`
- non-JSON, or shape mismatch
- **`version != 1`** → "has an unsupported native version"
- `entries.len() > SESSION_INDEX_ENTRY_LIMIT` (4_096, `adapter.rs:173`)
- `originalPath` present but whitespace-only
- `sessionId` not a UUID (`is_uuid`, `adapter.rs:2864-2872`: 8-4-4-4-12 ASCII hex)
- duplicate `sessionId`
- any of `fullPath`, `firstPrompt`, `created`, `modified`, `projectPath` blank after trim

`created`/`modified` → `native_timestamp` (verbatim ISO string). The whole raw document is retained as `native_snapshot: Value` (`sidecars.rs:1230-1239, 1339`).

```rust
pub struct SessionIndexEntrySnapshot {
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub native_session_id: String,
    pub full_path: String,
    pub file_mtime_ms: u64,
    pub first_prompt: String,
    pub summary: Option<String>,
    pub message_count: u64,
    pub created_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub modified_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub git_branch: String,
    pub project_path: String,
    pub is_sidechain: bool,
}

pub struct SessionIndexSnapshotFact {
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_project_key: String,
    pub native_version: u64,
    pub original_path: Option<String>,
    pub entries: Vec<SessionIndexEntrySnapshot>,
    pub native_snapshot: Value,
}
```
(`facts.rs:330-357`)

**Catalog discovery reads it independently** (`catalog_discovery.rs:366-403`, `read_index_document`): bounded stable read; only `entries[]` is walked, and only six fields are lifted — `sessionId` (must be UUID), `summary` **or else** `firstPrompt` as `title`, `created`, `modified`, `messageCount` (u64), `projectPath`. Index evidence introduces transcript-less sessions with `ProjectAssociationBasis::NativeProjectIndex` / `AssociationQuality::NativeClaimed`, and never overwrites the exact session-directory association (`catalog_discovery.rs:275-317`). The first entry's `projectPath` also names the project's `display_path` (`catalog_discovery.rs:168-184`).

**Legacy reader:** `read_sessions_index` (`project_parser.rs:495-501`) into `SessionsIndex` / `SessionIndexEntry` (`types/project.rs:13-57`) — same field set but *all* fields `#[serde(default)]`, `file_mtime: f64`, and a documented `Option<String>` for `summary` to preserve the NULL-vs-`''` distinction. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_SESSIONS_INDEX` (`fingerprint.rs:69-70`, `:526-534`). `src/claude/session_metadata.rs:49-55` supplies `normalize_first_prompt`, which rewrites synthetic index prompts (`<local-command-caveat>`, `<local-command-stdout>`, `<command-name>`, `<command-message>`, `<task-notification>`, `<system-reminder>`, `<ide_opened_file>`, `<ide_selection>` — `session_metadata.rs:12-21`) to the literal `"No prompt"`.

## 8. file-history — content blobs (`file-history-blobs`)

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include `file-history/*/*@v*` → `~/.claude/file-history/{sessionUuid}/{hash}@v{version}`
- `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_STREAM = "file-history-blobs"` (`adapter.rs:121`), `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_DECODER = "claude-file-history-blob"` (`adapter.rs:137`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:163`; spec `adapter.rs:501-519`)
- Entity scope `Custom("artifact")`, `Canonical`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Scope relation `file-artifacts-from-evidence`, locator `file-history/{native-session-id}/{backup-name}`, identity inputs `["native-session-id","backup-name","artifact-version"]` (`adapter.rs:3723-3730`)

**Filename → IDs** (`ClaudeArtifactContentContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1328-1355`): 3 components, `components[0] == "file-history"`, `components[1]` must be a UUID, `components[2]` parsed by `parse_artifact_file_name`.

```rust
fn parse_artifact_file_name(file_name: &str) -> Option<(String, u64)> {
    let (native_file_hash, version_text) = file_name.rsplit_once("@v")?;
    if native_file_hash.is_empty()
        || !native_file_hash
            .bytes()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&byte))
    {
        return None;
    }
    let version = version_text
        .parse::<u64>()
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| *value > 0)?;
    (version.to_string() == version_text).then(|| (native_file_hash.to_string(), version))
}
```
(`adapter.rs:2719-2733`) — hash must be **lowercase hex only** (uppercase rejected), version must be a canonical positive integer (`@v01` rejected).

```rust
struct ClaudeArtifactContentContext {
    native_session_id: String,
    native_artifact_id: String,   // the whole "{hash}@v{n}" file name
    native_file_hash: String,
    version: u64,
}
```

**Record type:** opaque bytes. `decode_artifact_content` (`sidecars.rs:911-960`) does no parsing at all — the payload is stored as `Vec<u8>`, base64-encoded on serialization.

```rust
pub struct ArtifactContentFact {
    pub artifact: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_artifact: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_session: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    pub native_artifact_id: String,
    pub native_file_hash: String,
    pub version: u64,
    #[serde(with = "base64_bytes")]
    pub content: Vec<u8>,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2401-2417`)

Artifact native key (`artifact_native_key`, `adapter.rs:2754-2786`): `[session_id, "named-backup", artifact_id]` when a backup name exists, else `[session_id, "not-captured", tracking_path, version_be, backup_time]`.

### 8b. file-history *metadata* — carried on transcript records

The tracked-file bookkeeping is not a separate on-disk file: it rides in the session JSONL as records with `type: "file-history-snapshot"` / `"file-history-delta"` (`artifact_metadata_snapshot`, `adapter.rs:2324-2478`).

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactCheckpointDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    pub(super) timestamp: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) tracked_file_backups: BTreeMap<String, ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactSnapshotDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) is_snapshot_update: bool,
    pub(super) snapshot: ClaudeArtifactCheckpointDocument,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactDeltaDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    pub(super) snapshot_message_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) timestamp: Option<String>,
    pub(super) tracking_path: String,
    pub(super) backup: ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument {
    pub(super) backup_file_name: Value,
    pub(super) version: u64,
    pub(super) backup_time: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) real_parent_dir: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:147-184`)

`backupFileName` is typed `Value` because it is tri-state: a `String` → `ArtifactCapture::ContentExpected` (and its `@v` version must equal `backup.version`, else error), `Null` → `ArtifactCapture::NotCaptured`, anything else → error (`adapter.rs:2403-2426`). Bound `ARTIFACT_METADATA_LIMIT = 512` entries (`adapter.rs:172`). Produces `ArtifactMetadataSnapshotFact` / `ArtifactMetadataEntry` (`facts.rs:2256-2289`).

**Legacy reader:** `read_file_history` (`project_parser.rs:917-968`) walks `file-history/{sessionId}/` with regex `^([0-9a-f]+)@v(\d+)$` (`fingerprint.rs:103-105` — this one *does* accept non-canonical versions like `@v01`), reads content as a UTF-8 string, sorts by file name for cross-engine determinism, → `FileHistorySession { session_id, snapshots: Vec<FileHistorySnapshotFile { hash, version, file_name, content, size }> }` (`types/artifacts.rs:17-34`). `FileBackupEntry { backup_file_name: Option<String>, version: u64, backup_time: String }` at `types/session.rs:241-245`. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_FILE_HISTORY` (`fingerprint.rs:75-76`, `:415`).

## 9. Memory — `project-memory-documents`

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/memory/*.md` → `~/.claude/projects/{projectSlug}/memory/{document}.md`, with `MEMORY.md` flagged as the index
- `PROJECT_MEMORY_STREAM = "project-memory-documents"` (`adapter.rs:125`), `PROJECT_MEMORY_DECODER = "claude-project-memory"` (`adapter.rs:141`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `PROJECT_MEMORY_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:166`; spec `adapter.rs:575-593`)
- Entity scope `Project`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeProjectMemoryContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1482-1507`): exactly 3 components, `components[0]` non-empty, `components[1] == "memory"`; file name must end in `.md`, must not be exactly `".md"`, and must contain no `/` or `\`.

```rust
struct ClaudeProjectMemoryContext {
    project_slug: String,
    native_document_path: String,   // always "memory/{file}"
    is_index: bool,                 // file_name == "MEMORY.md"
}
```

**Record type:** raw UTF-8 Markdown; non-UTF-8 → `preserve_unknown` kind `"project_memory_document"` (`sidecars.rs:1354-1365`).

**Fields** (`decode_project_memory_document`, `sidecars.rs:1345-1404`): no schema. `title` = `first_markdown_heading(&content)` falling back to the file stem (the `.md` strip is an `expect` guaranteed by the context validator, `sidecars.rs:1366-1373`). Document native key = `[project_slug, native_document_path]`.

```rust
pub struct ProjectMemoryDocumentFact {
    pub document: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_project_key: String,
    pub native_document_path: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub content: String,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
    pub is_index: bool,
}
```
(`facts.rs:362-372`)

**Legacy reader:** `read_project_memory` (`project_parser.rs:640-643`) reads **only** `projects/{slug}/memory/MEMORY.md` → `ProjectMemory { project_slug, content }` (`types/project.rs:123-128`). Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_MEMORY`, also MEMORY.md-only (`fingerprint.rs:67-68`, `:540-557`).

## 10. Settings — `interpretation-settings`

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include **exactly** `["settings.json", "settings.local.json"]` → `~/.claude/settings.json`, `~/.claude/settings.local.json`
- `INTERPRETATION_SETTINGS_STREAM = "interpretation-settings"` (`adapter.rs:127`), `INTERPRETATION_SETTINGS_DECODER = "claude-interpretation-settings"` (`adapter.rs:143`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `INTERPRETATION_SETTINGS_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:168`; spec `adapter.rs:613-634`)
- Entity scope `Instance`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`

**Filename → layer** (`ClaudeInterpretationSettingsContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1417-1440`): must be a single root-level component; `"settings.json" → InterpretationSettingsLayer::Global`, `"settings.local.json" → Local`, anything else is a hard path error. Negative cases asserted in tests: `settings.json.bak`, `project/settings.json`, **`managed-settings.json`** (`adapter.rs:4320-4323`).

**Managed / policy settings are explicitly NOT modeled** — `facts.rs:392-396`: *"The common pack deliberately models only the source instance's global and local documents. Managed policy, command-line overrides, and project roots outside the configured source instance are not inferred."*

**Record type:** a JSON object, decoded by an **allowlist** (`decode_interpretation_settings_snapshot`, `sidecars.rs:1549-1628`), not by serde derive. Root must be an object; `permissions` must be an object if present.

| Setting key | Rust field | Type | Reader |
|---|---|---|---|
| `agent` | `agent` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` |
| `model` | `model` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` |
| `effortLevel` | `effort_level` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` |
| `plansDirectory` | `plans_directory` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` |
| `alwaysThinkingEnabled` | `always_thinking_enabled` | `Option<bool>` | `optional_settings_bool` |
| `autoCompactEnabled` | `auto_compact_enabled` | `Option<bool>` | `optional_settings_bool` |
| `skipAutoPermissionPrompt` | `skip_auto_permission_prompt` | `Option<bool>` | `optional_settings_bool` |
| `permissions.defaultMode` | `permission_default_mode` | `Option<String>` | `optional_nested_settings_string` |
| `permissions.disableBypassPermissionsMode` | `disable_bypass_permissions_mode` | `Option<String>` | nested string |
| `permissions.disableAutoMode` | `disable_auto_mode` | `Option<String>` | nested string |
| `permissions.allow` | `permission_allow` | `Option<Vec<String>>` | `optional_nested_settings_string_array` |
| `permissions.ask` | `permission_ask` | `Option<Vec<String>>` | nested string array |
| `permissions.deny` | `permission_deny` | `Option<Vec<String>>` | nested string array |
| `enabledPlugins` | `enabled_plugins` | `Option<BTreeMap<String, bool>>` | `optional_settings_bool_map` |
| `hooks` | `hook_events` | `Option<BTreeMap<String, HookEventSummary>>` | `optional_hook_event_summaries` |

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct InterpretationSettingsSnapshot {
    pub agent: Option<String>,
    pub model: Option<String>,
    pub effort_level: Option<String>,
    pub plans_directory: Option<String>,
    pub always_thinking_enabled: Option<bool>,
    pub auto_compact_enabled: Option<bool>,
    pub skip_auto_permission_prompt: Option<bool>,
    pub permission_default_mode: Option<String>,
    pub disable_bypass_permissions_mode: Option<String>,
    pub disable_auto_mode: Option<String>,
    pub permission_allow: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub permission_ask: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub permission_deny: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub enabled_plugins: Option<BTreeMap<String, bool>>,
    pub hook_events: Option<BTreeMap<String, HookEventSummary>>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:426-443`)

**Hooks** (`optional_hook_event_summaries`, `sidecars.rs:1741-1802`) are deliberately **redacted to counts only**. `hooks` must be an object of `event → Array<matcher>`; each matcher must be an object with a `hooks` array. Nothing from the hook bodies (command, prompt, URL, matcher pattern, agent) is retained:
```rust
pub struct HookEventSummary {
    pub declared_matcher_count: u64,
    pub declared_hook_count: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:414-418`; comment at `:412-413`: *"Counts describe declarations, not successful hook executions."*)

**Plugins** are represented only as the `enabledPlugins` name→bool map. **Skills, marketplaces, statusline, env, and all other native keys are intentionally not represented** (`facts.rs:420-425`).

**Bounds & failure codes:**
- `SETTINGS_COLLECTION_LIMIT = 4_096` (`adapter.rs:174`) on each array, on `enabledPlugins`, on hook events, matchers, and total hooks per event
- `SETTINGS_STRING_MAX_BYTES = 16 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:175`), enforced by `validate_settings_string` (`sidecars.rs:1804-1814`)
- Codes: `"claude_settings_invalid_json"` (with line/column), `"claude_settings_invalid_shape"`, `"claude_settings_bounds"` (`sidecars.rs:1527-1560`)

**Invalid documents still emit a fact** (`decode_interpretation_settings`, `sidecars.rs:1465-1525`) with `document_status: Invalid`, `settings: None`, `error_code: Some(code)`, disposition `PreservedUnknown`, plus an `AdapterDiagnostic { class: RecordPermanent }`. The doc comment at `facts.rs:445-450` explains why: an invalid file must never become an empty or last-known-good permission layer.

```rust
pub struct InterpretationSettingsFact {
    pub document: EntityKey,
    pub scope: EntityKey,
    pub layer: InterpretationSettingsLayer,
    pub native_document_path: String,
    pub document_status: InterpretationSettingsDocumentStatus,
    pub settings: Option<InterpretationSettingsSnapshot>,
    pub error_code: Option<String>,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:451-460`; `InterpretationSettingsLayer` `{Global, Local}` at `:397-402`, `InterpretationSettingsDocumentStatus` `{Valid, Invalid}` at `:405-410`)

**Support probe** also reads `settings.json` (`support_probe.rs:21-30`), bounded to 1 MiB, and only checks that it parses to a JSON object → marker `"settings.schema-shape"`.

## 11. Workflows — runs (`workflow-runs`)

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/workflows/wf_*.json` → `~/.claude/projects/{slug}/{sessionUuid}/workflows/wf_{id}.json`
- `WORKFLOW_RUN_STREAM = "workflow-runs"` (`adapter.rs:122`), `WORKFLOW_RUN_DECODER = "claude-workflow-run"` (`adapter.rs:138`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `WORKFLOW_RUN_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:164`; spec `adapter.rs:520-538`)
- Entity scope `Custom("workflow")`, `Canonical`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Locator `{project-key}/{native-session-id}/workflows`, `relative_selector: Some("wf_*.json")` (`adapter.rs:3924-3932`)

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeWorkflowContext::run_from_path`, `adapter.rs:1515-1532` + `validate`, `:1554-1580`): exactly 4 components, `components[2] == "workflows"`, strip `.json`. Then: project slug non-empty, session must be a UUID, and workflow id must **start with `wf_` and have a non-empty suffix**.

```rust
struct ClaudeWorkflowContext {
    project_slug: String,
    native_session_id: String,
    native_workflow_id: String,
}
```

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeWorkflowRunDocument {
    pub(super) run_id: String,
    pub(super) timestamp: String,
    pub(super) task_id: String,
    pub(super) script: String,
    pub(super) script_path: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) args: Option<String>,
    pub(super) agent_count: u64,
    pub(super) duration_ms: u64,
    pub(super) summary: String,
    pub(super) workflow_name: String,
    pub(super) status: String,
    pub(super) start_time: u64,
    pub(super) default_model: String,
    pub(super) total_tokens: u64,
    pub(super) total_tool_calls: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) error: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:186-207`)

**JSON keys:** `runId`, `timestamp`, `taskId`, `script`, `scriptPath`, `args` (Option), `agentCount` (u64), `durationMs` (u64), `summary`, `workflowName`, `status`, `startTime` (u64 epoch-ms), `defaultModel`, `totalTokens` (u64), `totalToolCalls` (u64), `error` (Option). Only `args` and `error` are optional.

**Decode** (`decode_workflow_run`, `sidecars.rs:962-1067`):
- absent → `IgnoredKnown`
- `runId != context.native_workflow_id` → contract loss ("payload runId does not match the source file name")
- these eight must be non-blank or contract loss: `taskId`, `workflowName`, `status`, `defaultModel`, `script`, `scriptPath`, `summary`, `timestamp` (`sidecars.rs:1002-1020`)
- `startTime` must fit `i64`
- `started_at` from `epoch_millis_timestamp(start_time)`; **`finished_at` from `native_timestamp(&timestamp)`** — the `timestamp` field is treated as the completion time
- `status` normalized by `workflow_status` (`adapter.rs:2808-2817`): `pending|queued→Pending`, `running|in_progress→Running`, `completed→Succeeded`, `failed→Failed`, `cancelled|canceled|killed→Cancelled`, else `Other`
- entire raw document kept as `native_snapshot: Value`

Workflow native key = `[session_id, workflow_id]` (`adapter.rs:2788-2793`).

```rust
pub struct WorkflowSnapshotFact {
    pub workflow: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_workflow_id: String,
    pub native_task_id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub native_status: String,
    pub status: WorkflowStatus,
    pub default_model: String,
    pub script: String,
    pub script_path: String,
    pub args: Option<String>,
    pub summary: String,
    pub error: Option<String>,
    pub started_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub finished_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub duration_ms: u64,
    pub agent_count: u64,
    pub total_tokens: u64,
    pub total_tool_calls: u64,
    pub native_snapshot: Value,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2476-2499`; `WorkflowStatus` at `:2463-2471`)

**Legacy reader:** `read_workflows` (`project_parser.rs:764-822`) is far looser — accepts any `wf_*.json`, falls back to the filename when `runId` is absent, reads counters as `f64`, and additionally computes `subagent_count` by counting `subagents/workflows/{wf}/agent-*.jsonl` (`project_parser.rs:823-841`) → `WorkflowRun` (`types/workflow.rs:11-27`).

## 12. Workflows — journal (`workflow-journals`)

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/subagents/workflows/*/journal.jsonl` → `~/.claude/projects/{slug}/{sessionUuid}/subagents/workflows/wf_{id}/journal.jsonl`
- `WORKFLOW_JOURNAL_STREAM = "workflow-journals"` (`adapter.rs:123`), `WORKFLOW_JOURNAL_DECODER = "claude-workflow-journal"` (`adapter.rs:139`)
- Driver **`AppendDelimited(AppendDelimitedConfig::json_lines())`** with `ConsistencyPolicy::IncrementalCursor` — the only non-transcript append stream (`adapter.rs:539-555`)
- Entity scope `Custom("workflow_member")`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`
- Locator `{project-key}/{native-session-id}/subagents/workflows/{observed-workflow-id}/journal.jsonl` (`adapter.rs:3933-3941`)

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeWorkflowContext::journal_from_path`, `adapter.rs:1534-1552`): exactly 6 components with `components[2] == "subagents"`, `components[3] == "workflows"`, `components[5] == "journal.jsonl"`; workflow id is `components[4]`, then the same `validate` (UUID session, `wf_` prefix).

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeWorkflowJournalDocument {
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub(super) kind: String,
    pub(super) agent_id: String,
    pub(super) key: String,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:209-216`)

**JSON keys per line:** `type` (String, required), `agentId` (required non-empty), `key` (required non-empty), and `result` — read out of the raw `Value`, not the struct.

**Decode** (`decode_workflow_journal`, `sidecars.rs:1069-1219`) — the `type`/`result` pairing is strict (`sidecars.rs:1116-1145`):
- `"started"` **and no `result` key** → `WorkflowMemberEventKind::Started`, `result: None`
- `"result"` → `WorkflowMemberEventKind::Result`, `result: Some(value["result"])`; missing `result` → contract loss
- `"started"` **with** a `result` key → contract loss ("started record unexpectedly contains a result value")
- any other `type` → contract loss ("record has an unsupported event type")

Member native key = `workflow_native_key(context) ++ agent_id`. Child-run native key = `format!("{session}\0{workflowId}\0{agentId}")` (`sidecars.rs:1151-1153`). Also emits an `ActorAffiliationRevisionFact` with `dimension: ActorAffiliationDimension::Workflow`, `state: Present` (`sidecars.rs:1202-1217`).

```rust
pub struct WorkflowMemberEventFact {
    pub workflow: EntityKey,
    pub member: EntityKey,
    pub child_run: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_workflow_id: String,
    pub native_agent_id: String,
    pub native_event_key: String,
    pub kind: WorkflowMemberEventKind,
    pub result: Option<Value>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2510-2522`; `WorkflowMemberEventKind {Started, Result}` at `:2501-2505`)

**Legacy reader:** `read_workflow_journal` (`project_parser.rs:843-858`) collects every parseable line as a raw `Value` into `WorkflowRun.journal`. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_WORKFLOW_JOURNAL` (`fingerprint.rs:81-82`, `:644-652`).

## 13. Persisted tool results — `persisted-tool-results`

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/tool-results/*.txt` → `~/.claude/projects/{slug}/{sessionUuid}/tool-results/{toolUseId}.txt`
- `PERSISTED_TOOL_RESULT_STREAM = "persisted-tool-results"` (`adapter.rs:126`), `PERSISTED_TOOL_RESULT_DECODER = "claude-persisted-tool-result"` (`adapter.rs:142`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `PERSISTED_TOOL_RESULT_MAX_BYTES = 16 * 1024 * 1024` — the largest bound in the adapter (`adapter.rs:167`; spec `adapter.rs:594-612`)
- Entity scope `Custom("persisted_tool_result")`, authority **`Supplemental`**, `Interactive`

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudePersistedToolResultContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1447-1475`): exactly 4 components, `components[0]` non-empty, `components[1]` must be a UUID, `components[2] == "tool-results"`; strip `.txt` from `components[3]` → `native_tool_use_id` (non-empty). `native_document_path` is rebuilt as `format!("tool-results/{}", components[3])`.

```rust
struct ClaudePersistedToolResultContext {
    project_slug: String,
    native_session_id: String,
    native_tool_use_id: String,
    native_document_path: String,
}
```

**Record type:** raw UTF-8 text; non-UTF-8 → `preserve_unknown` kind `"persisted_tool_result"` (`sidecars.rs:1415-1426`). No field parsing; result native key = `[session_id, tool_use_id]`.

```rust
pub struct PersistedToolResultFact {
    pub result: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_project_key: String,
    pub native_session_id: String,
    pub native_tool_use_id: String,
    pub native_document_path: String,
    pub content: String,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:379-390`; doc comment at `:374-378` notes the file is *not* run or message evidence — correlation happens in the common projector via the session-scoped tool id)

**Legacy reader:** `read_tool_results` (`project_parser.rs:888-913`) → `PersistedToolResult { tool_use_id, content }` (`types/project.rs:112-117`). Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_TOOL_RESULT` (`fingerprint.rs:65-66`, `:711-748`).

## 14. Subagent metadata sidecar — `subagent-metadata`

Non-transcript sidecar beside each subagent JSONL.

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/subagents/**/agent-*.meta.json`
- `SUBAGENT_META_STREAM = "subagent-metadata"` (`adapter.rs:114`), `SUBAGENT_META_DECODER = "claude-subagent-metadata"` (`adapter.rs:130`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `SUBAGENT_META_MAX_BYTES = 64 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:156`; spec `adapter.rs:367-385`)
- Entity scope `Run`, authority `Supplemental`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Locator `{actor-transcript-object}.meta.json` (`adapter.rs:3851-3859`)

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeTranscriptContext::subagent_object(path, ".meta.json")`, `adapter.rs:1062-1087`): ≥ 4 components, `components[2] == "subagents"`; file name must be `agent-{id}.meta.json` with non-empty id; `workflow_id` is found by scanning component pairs for `("workflows", next)` (`adapter.rs:1078-1080`). Run native key = `"{session}\0{workflow_or_empty}\0{agent}"` (`adapter.rs:1093-1103`).

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeSubagentMetadataDocument {
    pub(super) agent_type: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) description: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) name: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) team_name: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) spawn_depth: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) worktree_path: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) tool_use_id: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:13-29`)

**JSON keys:** `agentType` (required non-empty, else `PreservedUnknown`), `description`, `name`, `teamName`, `spawnDepth` (Option<u32>), `worktreePath`, `toolUseId`.

**Decode** (`decode_subagent_metadata`, `adapter.rs:1718-1793`): emits `DelegationMetadataFact`; and when **both** `teamName` and `name` are non-empty, additionally emits a `Team`-dimension `ActorAffiliationRevisionFact` for the child run — this is the second on-disk source of team membership besides `teams/*/config.json`.

```rust
pub struct DelegationMetadataFact {
    pub child_run: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub native_child_id: String,
    pub agent_type: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub spawn_depth: Option<u32>,
    pub worktree_path: Option<String>,
    pub native_task_id: Option<String>,   // from toolUseId
}
```
(`facts.rs:2056-2066`)

**Legacy reader:** `read_one_subagent` (`project_parser.rs:747-750`) opens the sibling via `path.with_extension("meta.json")` → `SubagentMeta { agent_type: String, description: Option<String>, name: Option<String>, spawn_depth: Option<f64>, worktree_path: Option<String> }` (`types/project.rs:93-106`) — note **no `team_name` and no `tool_use_id`** in the legacy shape, and `spawn_depth` is `f64` there vs `u32` in the adapter. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_SUBAGENT_META` (`fingerprint.rs:79-80`, `:699-708`).

## 15. Catalog discovery — the projects tree itself

`src/claude/catalog_discovery.rs` derives the full catalog from filesystem layout with **no transcript bytes required** (module doc `:1-25`):

```text
<projects>/<project-slug>/                     -> a project
<projects>/<project-slug>/<uuid>.jsonl         -> a transcript-backed session
<projects>/<project-slug>/<uuid>/              -> a session with side data
<projects>/<project-slug>/sessions-index.json  -> sessions the agent knows about
```

- Scan is pinned to `max_depth: 2` regardless of `limits` (`catalog_discovery.rs:69-77`)
- `catalog_selection` (`:250-270`) includes depth-2 directories whose name is a UUID (recorded, **never descended into** — so a deep `subagents/` or `workflows/` tree costs nothing), plus depth-2 files named `sessions-index.json` or ending `.jsonl`
- When there is no session index, the project's `display_path` comes from **one bounded transcript-head read** (`read_transcript_cwd`, `:212-219`) — splits the prefix on `\n`, parses each complete line, takes the first record's `cwd`. The newest transcript by `(modified_ns, relative_path)` is chosen (`:141-155`), because *"the slug cannot be reversed safely because `-` may be either a path separator or part of a directory name"* (`:186-191`)
- `IndexEntry` reduction and association merging: `:46-54`, `:275-364`

---

## Streams the crate does **NOT** parse

Verified absent from `src/` (repo-wide grep):

| Asked-about stream | Status |
|---|---|
| `~/.claude.json` | **Not parsed.** No occurrence anywhere in `src/`. |
| `~/.claude/history.jsonl` | **Not parsed.** The only `history.jsonl` hits are Grok's `chat_history.jsonl` and a synthetic test fixture in `src/adapter/scope.rs:961,977`. |
| `shell-snapshots/` | **Not parsed.** Zero occurrences. |
| Managed / policy settings (`managed-settings.json`) | **Deliberately excluded** — appears only as a rejected path in a negative test (`adapter.rs:4323`), and `facts.rs:392-396` states managed policy is not inferred. |
| `CLAUDE.md` discovery | **Not parsed.** Zero occurrences. Project memory is `projects/*/memory/*.md` only. |
| `plugins/` | **Not scanned.** Only the `enabledPlugins` name→bool map inside settings. `fingerprint.rs:406-408` explicitly names `plugins/`, `shell/`, `ide/`, `statsig/` as dirs the allow-listed walk avoids. |
| Skills | **Not parsed at all** — no on-disk skill reader, and skills are not in the settings allowlist. |
| Hooks | **Only as redacted counts** inside `settings.json`/`settings.local.json` (`HookEventSummary`). No hook script, matcher pattern, or command text is retained. |
| `config.json` | Only `teams/{team}/config.json`. There is no generic `~/.claude/config.json` reader. |
| `tasks/*/.lock`, `tasks/*/.highwatermark` | Read by the **legacy** parser and fingerprinter only (`project_parser.rs:1017-1040`, `fingerprint.rs:73-74`); the ADS adapter's `task-items` stream never touches them. |

---

## Cross-cutting notes

- **Declared source schema versions** (`adapter.rs:228-243`): `claude-code-jsonl-v1`, `-subagent-meta-v1`, `-team-config-v1`, `-team-inbox-v2`, `-active-session-v1`, `-todo-v1`, `-task-item-v1`, `-plan-v1`, `-file-history-v1`, `-workflow-v1`, `-session-index-v1`, `-project-memory-v1`, `-persisted-tool-result-v1`, `-interpretation-settings-v1`. Contract version 23 (`adapter.rs:212`).
- **Two timestamp conventions.** `epoch_millis_timestamp(i64)` converts epoch-ms to ISO-8601 (`sidecars.rs:1960-1965`) and is used for team `createdAt`/`joinedAt`, presence `startedAt`/`updatedAt`/`statusUpdatedAt`, and workflow `startTime`. `native_timestamp(&str)` keeps the native string verbatim (`adapter.rs:2829-2834`) and is used for inbox `timestamp`, session-index `created`/`modified`, workflow `timestamp`, and artifact `backupTime`. Both stamp `TimestampQuality::NativeExact`.
- **Entity native keys** are always built with `push_key_component` — an 8-byte big-endian length prefix per component (`adapter.rs:2714-2717`) — so component boundaries are unambiguous and no separator collision is possible.
- **Failure discipline.** Malformed JSON, out-of-bounds collections, and semantic contract violations never drop the record: they route through `preserve_unknown` with a per-stream native kind (`"team_config"`, `"team_inbox"`, `"active_session"`, `"todo_snapshot"`, `"task_item"`, `"plan_document"`, `"session_index"`, `"workflow_run"`, `"workflow_journal"`, `"project_memory_document"`, `"persisted_tool_result"`, `"subagent_metadata"`) and return `DecodeDisposition::PreservedUnknown`.
- **Absent-record handling.** Streams whose objects can vanish (`active_session`, `artifact_content`, `workflow_run`, `workflow_journal`, `session_index`, `project_memory`, `persisted_tool_result`, `interpretation_settings`) short-circuit on `record.state == SourceRecordState::Absent` and return `IgnoredKnown`. Todos, tasks, plans, team configs, and team inboxes have no such guard — deletion is handled by `DeletionPolicy::MirrorSource`, which every stream declares.
- **`src/claude/session_metadata.rs`** is transcript projection, not an on-disk stream, but its `normalize_first_prompt` (`:49-55`) is what the session-index `firstPrompt` display path runs through; `extract_human_prompt` (`:57-75`) filters `isMeta`/`isSidechain`/`isCompactSummary`/`isVisibleInTranscriptOnly` records and truncates to 200 UTF-16 units, and `project_session_metadata` (`:77-99`) handles `ai-title` (`aiTitle`) and `custom-title` (`customTitle`) records.
# Claude Code non-transcript on-disk streams parsed by `spaghetti-napi`

Three independent readers touch Claude's on-disk tree:

| Reader | File | Role |
|---|---|---|
| **ADS adapter** (primary) | `/Users/jamesyong/Projects/project100/p008/spaghetti/crates/spaghetti-napi/src/claude/adapter.rs` + `src/claude/adapter/sidecars.rs` | Declares 16 `StreamSpec`s, 14 non-transcript; decodes each into typed `Fact`s |
| **Catalog discovery** | `src/claude/catalog_discovery.rs` | Bounded projects-root scan; reads `sessions-index.json` + one transcript head for cwd |
| **Legacy parser / fingerprinter** | `src/claude/project_parser.rs`, `src/claude/fingerprint.rs` | Older direct-filesystem path with its own structs in `src/claude/types/` |

Roots the adapter authorizes (`adapter.rs:296-313`):

```rust
roots: vec![
    SourceRoot { name: "home".to_string(),     path: canonical.clone() },          // ~/.claude
    SourceRoot { name: "projects".to_string(), path: canonical.join("projects") },
    SourceRoot { name: "teams".to_string(),    path: canonical.join("teams") },
    SourceRoot { name: "sessions".to_string(), path: canonical.join("sessions") },
],
```

Streams whose `selector.root_name` is not in the instance's authorized roots are dropped (`adapter.rs:636-642`).

## 1. Todos — `todo-snapshots`

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include `todos/*-agent-*.json` → `~/.claude/todos/{sessionId}-agent-{agentId}.json`
- `TODO_STREAM = "todo-snapshots"` (`adapter.rs:118`), decoder `TODO_DECODER = "claude-todo-snapshot"` (`adapter.rs:134`)
- Driver `ReplaceDocument`, `TODO_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:160`; spec `adapter.rs:444-462`)
- Entity scope `Custom("task_collection")`, authority `Canonical`, priority `Interactive`
- Scope-program locator: `todos/{native-session-id}-agent-{native-actor-id}.json`, identity inputs `["native-session-id","native-actor-id"]` (`adapter.rs:3897-3905`)

**Filename → IDs** (`ClaudeTodoContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1215-1243`): exactly 2 path components, `components[0] == "todos"`; strip `.json`; then **`stem.rsplit_once("-agent-")`** → `(native_session_id, native_agent_id)`. Both must be non-empty. Note `rsplit_once`, so an agent id containing `-agent-` binds to the **last** delimiter.

```rust
struct ClaudeTodoContext {
    native_session_id: String,
    native_agent_id: String,
}
```

**Record type:** a JSON **array** of todo items.

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeTodoItemDocument {
    pub(super) content: String,
    pub(super) status: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) active_form: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:121-128`)

**Fields parsed:** `content: String` (required, non-empty else contract loss), `status: String` (required, non-empty), `activeForm → active_form: Option<String>`.

**Decode** (`decode_todo_snapshot`, `sidecars.rs:648-777`):
- Bound: `TODO_ITEM_LIMIT = 4_096` items (`adapter.rs:171`); over-limit → `preserve_task_contract_loss`
- Task identity is content-derived: `blake3::hash(subject)` + a per-digest occurrence counter, appended to the collection key `["todo", session_id, agent_id]` (`sidecars.rs:675-717`)
- Emits `Fact::TaskSnapshot(TaskSnapshotFact { kind: TaskCollectionKind::TodoList, coverage: TaskSnapshotCoverage::Complete, native_collection_id: format!("{session}-agent-{agent}"), native_owner_id: Some(agent_id), session: Some(...), run: Some(...) only when agent_id == session_id, team: None })` (`sidecars.rs:736-768`)
- `native_task_id`, `description`, `native_owner`, `blocks`, `blocked_by` are all `None`/empty for todos
- `status` normalized by `task_status` (`sidecars.rs:1860-1867`): `"pending"→Pending`, `"in_progress"→InProgress`, `"completed"→Completed`, else `Other(String)`
- Also emits RFC-012C runtime facts via `emit_task_snapshot_runtime_facts` (`sidecars.rs:769-775`; helper at `runtime_facts.rs:1181-1215`), with actor-run resolution in `canonical_todo_actor_run` (`adapter.rs:2249-2262`): agent == session → root actor run; otherwise run native key `"{session}\0\0{agent}"`

**Target types:**
```rust
pub struct TaskItemSnapshot {
    pub task: EntityKey,
    pub native_task_id: Option<String>,
    pub subject: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub active_form: Option<String>,
    pub native_owner: Option<String>,
    pub status: TaskStatus,
    pub blocks: Vec<String>,
    pub blocked_by: Vec<String>,
}

pub struct TaskSnapshotFact {
    pub collection: EntityKey,
    pub session: Option<EntityKey>,
    pub run: Option<EntityKey>,
    pub team: Option<EntityKey>,
    pub native_collection_id: String,
    pub native_owner_id: Option<String>,
    pub kind: TaskCollectionKind,
    pub coverage: TaskSnapshotCoverage,
    pub items: Vec<TaskItemSnapshot>,
}
```
(`src/adapter/facts.rs:2196-2224`; `TaskCollectionKind` at `:2173-2178`, `TaskSnapshotCoverage` at `:2182-2186`, `TaskStatus` at `:2188-2194`)

**Legacy reader:** `project_parser.rs:972-1013` (`read_todos`) globs `todos/{sessionId}-agent-*.json`, parses `Vec<TodoItem>` into `TodoFile { session_id, agent_id, items }` (`types/artifacts.rs:40-47`; `TodoItem { content: String, status: String, active_form: Option<String> }` at `types/session.rs:322-328`). Fingerprinted as `CATEGORY_TODO` via regex `^(.+?)-agent-(.+)\.json$` — **non-greedy** first group, the opposite binding from the adapter's `rsplit_once` (`fingerprint.rs:108-109`, `:750-780`).

## 2. Tasks — `task-items`

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include `tasks/*/*.json` → `~/.claude/tasks/{collection}/{taskId}.json`
- `TASK_ITEM_STREAM = "task-items"` (`adapter.rs:119`), `TASK_ITEM_DECODER = "claude-task-item"` (`adapter.rs:135`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `TASK_ITEM_MAX_BYTES = 256 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:161`; spec `adapter.rs:463-481`)
- Entity scope `Custom("task")`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`
- Locator `tasks/{collection}/{task-id}.json`, identity inputs `["collection","task-id"]` (`adapter.rs:3915-3923`)

**Filename → IDs** (`ClaudeTaskItemContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1256-1283`): exactly 3 components, `components[0] == "tasks"`, `components[1]` non-empty; stem must be a **canonical positive integer** — parsed as `u64`, must be `> 0`, and `value.to_string()` must equal the stem (rejects `01`, `+1`, `1.0`).

```rust
struct ClaudeTaskItemContext {
    native_collection_id: String,
    native_task_id: String,
}
```

**Record type:** a single JSON object.

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeTaskItemDocument {
    pub(super) id: String,
    pub(super) subject: String,
    pub(super) description: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) active_form: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) owner: Option<String>,
    pub(super) status: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) blocks: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) blocked_by: Vec<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:130-145`)

**Fields:** `id: String` (required; must equal the filename stem or contract loss), `subject: String` (required non-empty), `description: String` (required, may be empty), `activeForm → active_form: Option<String>`, `owner: Option<String>`, `status: String` (required non-empty), `blocks: Vec<String>` (defaults `[]`), `blockedBy → blocked_by: Vec<String>` (defaults `[]`). Any dependency id that trims to empty → contract loss (`sidecars.rs:811-823`).

**Decode** (`decode_task_item`, `sidecars.rs:779-871`): emits `TaskSnapshotFact` with `kind: TaskCollectionKind::NativeTaskList`, `coverage: TaskSnapshotCoverage::ItemDocument`, one item, and **`session: None, run: None, team: None`** — the comment at `sidecars.rs:842-845` is explicit that a task-directory name may be a session id, team name, or another scope, so it is deliberately left unjoined. Collection native key `["task-directory", collection_id]`.

**Legacy reader:** `project_parser.rs:1017-1040` (`read_task`) reads a *different* shape — keys on `tasks/{sessionId}/.lock` existence and `tasks/{sessionId}/.highwatermark` content (lenient `parseInt` semantics via `parse_leading_int`, `project_parser.rs:1046+`):
```rust
pub struct TaskEntry {
    pub task_id: String,
    #[serde(default)] pub has_highwatermark: bool,
    #[serde(default)] pub highwatermark: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)] pub lock_exists: bool,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] pub items: Option<Vec<TaskItem>>,
}
pub struct TaskItem {
    pub id: String, pub subject: String, pub description: String,
    pub active_form: Option<String>, pub owner: Option<String>,
    pub status: String,
    pub blocks: Option<Vec<String>>, pub blocked_by: Option<Vec<String>>,
}
```
(`types/artifacts.rs:53-83`). `.lock` / `.highwatermark` are fingerprinted as `CATEGORY_TASK` (`fingerprint.rs:73-74`, `:783+`) but **the ADS adapter never reads them**.

## 3. Plans — `plan-documents`

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include `plans/*.md` → `~/.claude/plans/{slug}.md`
- `PLAN_STREAM = "plan-documents"` (`adapter.rs:120`), `PLAN_DECODER = "claude-plan-document"` (`adapter.rs:136`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `PLAN_MAX_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:162`; spec `adapter.rs:482-500`)
- Entity scope `Custom("plan")`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`
- Locator `plans/{plan-slug}.md`, identity inputs `["plan-slug"]` (`adapter.rs:3906-3914`)

**Filename → ID** (`ClaudePlanContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1295-1313`): 2 components, `components[0] == "plans"`, strip `.md`, must be non-empty.

```rust
struct ClaudePlanContext { native_plan_id: String }
```

**Record type:** raw UTF-8 Markdown (not JSON). Non-UTF-8 → `preserve_unknown` with native kind `"plan_document"` (`sidecars.rs:879-890`).

**Fields parsed** (`decode_plan_document`, `sidecars.rs:873-909`): no schema — the whole file body is the payload. `title` comes from `first_markdown_heading(&content)` (`sidecars.rs:1869-1878`: first line starting with `#` followed by whitespace), falling back to `native_plan_id`. `size_bytes = record.payload.len()`. `source_time: None`.

```rust
pub struct PlanSnapshotFact {
    pub plan: EntityKey,
    pub native_plan_id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub content: String,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
    pub source_time: Option<QualifiedTimestamp>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2228-2236`)

Note: `emit_plan_runtime_facts` exists but is **not** called from the plan-document decoder; its only caller is inside the transcript path (`runtime_facts.rs:494`).

**Legacy reader:** `parse_plans` (`project_parser.rs:1230-1275`) → `PlanFile { slug, title, content, size }` (`types/artifacts.rs:89-97`), title from a `PLAN_TITLE` regex with an explicit CR-trim for CRLF parity. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_PLAN`, global not project-scoped (`fingerprint.rs:77-78`, `:425-451`).

## 4. Teams — config (`team-configs`)

**Path / glob:** root `teams`, include `*/config.json` → `~/.claude/teams/{teamName}/config.json`
- `TEAM_CONFIG_STREAM = "team-configs"` (`adapter.rs:115`), `TEAM_CONFIG_DECODER = "claude-team-config"` (`adapter.rs:131`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `TEAM_CONFIG_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:157`; spec `adapter.rs:386-404`)
- Entity scope `Custom("team")`, `Canonical`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Locator `{observed-team-name}/config.json`, identity inputs `["observed-team-name"]` (`adapter.rs:3870-3878`)

**Filename → ID** (`ClaudeTeamConfigContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1122-1134`): exactly 2 components, `components[1] == "config.json"`, `components[0]` non-empty → `native_team_id`. The **directory name is the team id**; the `name` field inside is a separate display name.

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeTeamConfigDocument {
    pub(super) name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) description: Option<String>,
    pub(super) created_at: i64,
    pub(super) lead_agent_id: String,
    pub(super) lead_session_id: String,
    pub(super) members: Vec<ClaudeTeamMemberDocument>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeTeamMemberDocument {
    pub(super) agent_id: String,
    pub(super) name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) agent_type: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) model: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) prompt: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) color: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) plan_mode_required: Option<bool>,
    pub(super) joined_at: i64,
    pub(super) tmux_pane_id: String,
    pub(super) cwd: String,
    pub(super) subscriptions: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) backend_type: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:31-64`)

**JSON key names** (camelCase): `name`, `description`, `createdAt` (epoch **ms**, `i64`), `leadAgentId`, `leadSessionId`, `members[]`; per member `agentId`, `name`, `agentType`, `model`, `prompt`, `color`, `planModeRequired`, `joinedAt` (epoch ms), `tmuxPaneId`, `cwd`, `subscriptions`, `backendType`.

**Decode** (`decode_team_config`, `sidecars.rs:244-374`) — validation gates, each producing `preserve_team_config_contract_loss`:
- `members.len() > TEAM_MEMBER_LIMIT` (256, `adapter.rs:169`)
- empty `name` / `leadAgentId` / `leadSessionId` / member `name` / member `agentId`
- duplicate member names ("member names are not unique")
- no member whose `agentId == leadAgentId` ("does not contain its declared lead member")
- more than one such member ("contains an ambiguous declared lead member")

Member identity is `team_member_key(team_id, member_name)` — **name-keyed, not agentId-keyed** (`sidecars.rs:1880-1895`). Timestamps go through `epoch_millis_timestamp` → ISO-8601 with `TimestampQuality::NativeExact` (`sidecars.rs:1960-1965`). Also emits an `ActorAffiliationRevisionFact` with `dimension: Team` for the lead session (`emit_team_affiliation`, `sidecars.rs:1907-1958`).

```rust
pub struct TeamMemberSnapshot {
    pub member: EntityKey,
    pub native_agent_id: String,
    pub native_name: String,
    pub agent_type: Option<String>,
    pub model: Option<String>,
    pub prompt: Option<String>,
    pub color: Option<String>,
    pub plan_mode_required: Option<bool>,
    pub joined_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub tmux_pane_id: String,
    pub cwd: String,
    pub subscriptions: Vec<String>,
    pub backend_type: Option<String>,
}

pub struct TeamSnapshotFact {
    pub team: EntityKey,
    pub native_team_id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub created_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub lead_member: Option<EntityKey>,
    pub native_lead_agent_id: String,
    pub lead_session: EntityKey,
    pub native_lead_session_id: String,
    pub members: Vec<TeamMemberSnapshot>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2085-2117`)

## 5. Teams — inboxes / messages (`team-inboxes`)

**Path / glob:** root `teams`, include `*/inboxes/*.json` → `~/.claude/teams/{teamName}/inboxes/{recipientName}.json`
- `TEAM_INBOX_STREAM = "team-inboxes"` (`adapter.rs:116`), `TEAM_INBOX_DECODER = "claude-team-inbox"` (`adapter.rs:132`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `TEAM_INBOX_MAX_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:158`; spec `adapter.rs:405-423`)
- Entity scope `Custom("team_inbox")`, `Canonical`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Locator `{observed-team-name}/inboxes/{observed-recipient}.json` (`adapter.rs:3879-3887`)
- Source schema version string: `"claude-code-team-inbox-v2"` (`adapter.rs:232`)

**Filename → IDs** (`ClaudeTeamInboxContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1147-1166`): 3 components, `components[1] == "inboxes"`, strip `.json` from `components[2]` → `native_recipient_name` (non-empty).

```rust
struct ClaudeTeamInboxContext {
    native_team_id: String,
    native_recipient_name: String,
}
```

**Record type:** a JSON **array** of messages. This struct is **not** `rename_all = "camelCase"` — keys are literal, with two explicit renames.

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub(super) struct ClaudeTeamInboxMessageDocument {
    pub(super) from: String,
    pub(super) text: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) summary: Option<String>,
    pub(super) timestamp: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) color: Option<String>,
    pub(super) read: bool,
    #[serde(default, rename = "msg_id")]
    pub(super) message_id: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default, rename = "msgV")]
    pub(super) message_version: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default, rename = "type")]
    pub(super) kind: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:66-82`)

**JSON keys:** `from` (String, required non-empty), `text` (String, required), `summary` (Option), `timestamp` (String, required non-empty, ISO string kept verbatim), `color` (Option), `read` (bool, **required**), `msg_id` (Option<String>), `msgV` (Option<u32>), `type` (Option<String>).

**Decode** (`decode_team_inbox`, `sidecars.rs:390-516`):
- Bound `TEAM_INBOX_MESSAGE_LIMIT = 4_096` (`adapter.rs:170`)
- Message identity: if `msg_id` present, key = `[team, recipient, "native-id", msg_id]`, and a duplicate `msg_id` in one file is contract loss. If absent, a **legacy fingerprint** — `blake3` over length-prefixed `(from, timestamp, text)` plus a per-digest occurrence counter — key = `[team, recipient, "legacy-fingerprint", digest, occurrence]` (`sidecars.rs:441-468`)
- Sender resolves to `team_member_key(team_id, from)`
- `source_time` uses `native_timestamp` (verbatim string, `NativeExact`), *not* epoch-ms conversion

```rust
pub struct TeamInboxMessageSnapshot {
    pub message: EntityKey,
    pub sender: EntityKey,
    pub native_message_id: Option<String>,
    pub native_kind: Option<String>,
    pub native_version: Option<u32>,
    pub native_sender_name: String,
    pub text: String,
    pub summary: Option<String>,
    pub color: Option<String>,
    pub source_time: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub read: bool,
}

pub struct TeamInboxSnapshotFact {
    pub inbox: EntityKey,
    pub team: EntityKey,
    pub recipient: EntityKey,
    pub native_team_id: String,
    pub native_recipient_name: String,
    pub messages: Vec<TeamInboxMessageSnapshot>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2119-2145`)

## 6. Sessions (active-session presence) — `active-sessions`

**Path / glob:** root `sessions`, include `*.json` → `~/.claude/sessions/{pid}.json`
- `ACTIVE_SESSION_STREAM = "active-sessions"` (`adapter.rs:117`), `ACTIVE_SESSION_DECODER = "claude-active-session"` (`adapter.rs:133`)
- Driver is **`DriverSpec::Presence(PresenceObjectConfig { include_content: true, max_content_bytes: ACTIVE_SESSION_MAX_BYTES })`**, `ACTIVE_SESSION_MAX_BYTES = 64 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:159`; spec `adapter.rs:424-443`)
- Entity scope `Custom("process_presence")`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`
- Locator `{observed-pid}.json`, identity inputs `["observed-pid","native-session-id"]` (`adapter.rs:3888-3896`)

**Filename → ID** (`ClaudeActiveSessionContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1178-1202`): exactly 1 component; strip `.json`; stem must be all ASCII digits; parse as `u32`; must be `> 0`.

```rust
struct ClaudeActiveSessionContext { native_pid: u32 }
```

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeActiveSessionDocument {
    pub(super) pid: u32,
    pub(super) session_id: String,
    pub(super) cwd: String,
    pub(super) started_at: i64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) kind: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) entrypoint: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) name: Option<String>,
    /// Deliberately decoded but not projected: observed values look like epoch
    /// milliseconds, but native transition semantics are not fixture-proven.
    #[serde(default, rename = "nameSince")]
    pub(super) _name_since: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) status: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) updated_at: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) status_updated_at: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) proc_start: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) version: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) peer_protocol: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) name_source: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) bridge_session_id: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) messaging_socket_path: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:84-119`)

**JSON keys:** `pid` (u32, required), `sessionId` (required non-empty), `cwd` (required non-empty), `startedAt` (i64 epoch-ms, required, ≥ 0), `kind`, `entrypoint`, `name`, `nameSince` (**decoded and discarded** — `_name_since`), `status`, `updatedAt` (Option<i64>, ≥ 0), `statusUpdatedAt` (Option<i64>, ≥ 0), `procStart` (Option<String>), `version`, `peerProtocol` (Option<u32>), `nameSource`, `bridgeSessionId`, `messagingSocketPath`.

**Decode** (`decode_active_session`, `sidecars.rs:532-632`):
- `record.state == SourceRecordState::Absent` → `IgnoredKnown` (process exited)
- `pid == 0 || pid != context.native_pid` → contract loss ("payload pid does not match the source file name")
- any negative epoch-ms → contract loss
- Presence identity key: `[pid_be_bytes, session_id]` then either `["proc-start", procStart]` when present, or `["session-start", startedAt_be_bytes]` (`sidecars.rs:578-590`) — so PID reuse with a different process start makes a new presence entity
- Emits `Fact::Presence(PresenceFact { .. })` plus `session` and `run` entity keys derived from `sessionId`

```rust
pub struct PresenceFact {
    pub presence: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub run: EntityKey,
    pub native_session_id: String,
    pub native_pid: u32,
    pub cwd: String,
    pub started_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub native_kind: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub native_status: Option<String>,
    pub updated_at: Option<QualifiedTimestamp>,
    pub status_updated_at: Option<QualifiedTimestamp>,
    pub native_process_started_at: Option<String>,
    pub version: Option<String>,
    pub peer_protocol: Option<u32>,
    pub name_source: Option<String>,
    pub bridge_session_id: Option<String>,
    pub messaging_socket_path: Option<String>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2150-2171`)

**Also read by the support probe** (`support_probe.rs:32-66`): `sessions/*.json` with an all-digit stem, bounded to 256 entries × 64 KiB; extracts only the `version` string (1..=256 bytes) as the `active-session.version` marker; more than one distinct version across roots sets `contradictory_markers`.

## 7. `projects/*/sessions-index.json` — `session-indexes`

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/sessions-index.json` → `~/.claude/projects/{projectSlug}/sessions-index.json`
- `SESSION_INDEX_STREAM = "session-indexes"` (`adapter.rs:124`), `SESSION_INDEX_DECODER = "claude-session-index"` (`adapter.rs:140`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `SESSION_INDEX_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:165`; spec `adapter.rs:556-574`)
- Entity scope `Project`, authority **`Supplemental`**, `Interactive`

**Filename → ID** (`ClaudeSessionIndexContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1374-1389`): exactly 2 components, `components[0]` non-empty, `components[1] == "sessions-index.json"` → `project_slug`.

```rust

pub(super) struct ClaudeSessionIndexDocument {
    pub(super) version: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) original_path: Option<String>,
    pub(super) entries: Vec<ClaudeSessionIndexEntryDocument>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeSessionIndexEntryDocument {
    pub(super) session_id: String,
    pub(super) full_path: String,
    pub(super) file_mtime: u64,
    pub(super) first_prompt: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) summary: Option<String>,
    pub(super) message_count: u64,
    pub(super) created: String,
    pub(super) modified: String,
    pub(super) git_branch: String,
    pub(super) project_path: String,
    pub(super) is_sidechain: bool,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:218-242`)

**JSON keys:** top level `version` (u64), `originalPath` (Option<String>), `entries[]`. Per entry: `sessionId`, `fullPath`, `fileMtime` (u64 ms), `firstPrompt`, `summary` (Option), `messageCount` (u64), `created`, `modified`, `gitBranch`, `projectPath`, `isSidechain` (bool). All non-`summary` entry fields are **required** (no `#[serde(default)]`).

**Decode** (`decode_session_index`, `sidecars.rs:1221-1343`) — contract-loss gates:
- absent record → `IgnoredKnown`
- non-JSON, or shape mismatch
- **`version != 1`** → "has an unsupported native version"
- `entries.len() > SESSION_INDEX_ENTRY_LIMIT` (4_096, `adapter.rs:173`)
- `originalPath` present but whitespace-only
- `sessionId` not a UUID (`is_uuid`, `adapter.rs:2864-2872`: 8-4-4-4-12 ASCII hex)
- duplicate `sessionId`
- any of `fullPath`, `firstPrompt`, `created`, `modified`, `projectPath` blank after trim

`created`/`modified` → `native_timestamp` (verbatim ISO string). The whole raw document is retained as `native_snapshot: Value` (`sidecars.rs:1230-1239, 1339`).

```rust
pub struct SessionIndexEntrySnapshot {
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub native_session_id: String,
    pub full_path: String,
    pub file_mtime_ms: u64,
    pub first_prompt: String,
    pub summary: Option<String>,
    pub message_count: u64,
    pub created_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub modified_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub git_branch: String,
    pub project_path: String,
    pub is_sidechain: bool,
}

pub struct SessionIndexSnapshotFact {
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_project_key: String,
    pub native_version: u64,
    pub original_path: Option<String>,
    pub entries: Vec<SessionIndexEntrySnapshot>,
    pub native_snapshot: Value,
}
```
(`facts.rs:330-357`)

**Catalog discovery reads it independently** (`catalog_discovery.rs:366-403`, `read_index_document`): bounded stable read; only `entries[]` is walked, and only six fields are lifted — `sessionId` (must be UUID), `summary` **or else** `firstPrompt` as `title`, `created`, `modified`, `messageCount` (u64), `projectPath`. Index evidence introduces transcript-less sessions with `ProjectAssociationBasis::NativeProjectIndex` / `AssociationQuality::NativeClaimed`, and never overwrites the exact session-directory association (`catalog_discovery.rs:275-317`). The first entry's `projectPath` also names the project's `display_path` (`catalog_discovery.rs:168-184`).

**Legacy reader:** `read_sessions_index` (`project_parser.rs:495-501`) into `SessionsIndex` / `SessionIndexEntry` (`types/project.rs:13-57`) — same field set but *all* fields `#[serde(default)]`, `file_mtime: f64`, and a documented `Option<String>` for `summary` to preserve the NULL-vs-`''` distinction. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_SESSIONS_INDEX` (`fingerprint.rs:69-70`, `:526-534`). `src/claude/session_metadata.rs:49-55` supplies `normalize_first_prompt`, which rewrites synthetic index prompts (`<local-command-caveat>`, `<local-command-stdout>`, `<command-name>`, `<command-message>`, `<task-notification>`, `<system-reminder>`, `<ide_opened_file>`, `<ide_selection>` — `session_metadata.rs:12-21`) to the literal `"No prompt"`.

## 8. file-history — content blobs (`file-history-blobs`)

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include `file-history/*/*@v*` → `~/.claude/file-history/{sessionUuid}/{hash}@v{version}`
- `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_STREAM = "file-history-blobs"` (`adapter.rs:121`), `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_DECODER = "claude-file-history-blob"` (`adapter.rs:137`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:163`; spec `adapter.rs:501-519`)
- Entity scope `Custom("artifact")`, `Canonical`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Scope relation `file-artifacts-from-evidence`, locator `file-history/{native-session-id}/{backup-name}`, identity inputs `["native-session-id","backup-name","artifact-version"]` (`adapter.rs:3723-3730`)

**Filename → IDs** (`ClaudeArtifactContentContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1328-1355`): 3 components, `components[0] == "file-history"`, `components[1]` must be a UUID, `components[2]` parsed by `parse_artifact_file_name`.

```rust
fn parse_artifact_file_name(file_name: &str) -> Option<(String, u64)> {
    let (native_file_hash, version_text) = file_name.rsplit_once("@v")?;
    if native_file_hash.is_empty()
        || !native_file_hash
            .bytes()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&byte))
    {
        return None;
    }
    let version = version_text
        .parse::<u64>()
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| *value > 0)?;
    (version.to_string() == version_text).then(|| (native_file_hash.to_string(), version))
}
```
(`adapter.rs:2719-2733`) — hash must be **lowercase hex only** (uppercase rejected), version must be a canonical positive integer (`@v01` rejected).

```rust
struct ClaudeArtifactContentContext {
    native_session_id: String,
    native_artifact_id: String,   // the whole "{hash}@v{n}" file name
    native_file_hash: String,
    version: u64,
}
```

**Record type:** opaque bytes. `decode_artifact_content` (`sidecars.rs:911-960`) does no parsing at all — the payload is stored as `Vec<u8>`, base64-encoded on serialization.

```rust
pub struct ArtifactContentFact {
    pub artifact: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_artifact: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_session: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    pub native_artifact_id: String,
    pub native_file_hash: String,
    pub version: u64,
    #[serde(with = "base64_bytes")]
    pub content: Vec<u8>,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2401-2417`)

Artifact native key (`artifact_native_key`, `adapter.rs:2754-2786`): `[session_id, "named-backup", artifact_id]` when a backup name exists, else `[session_id, "not-captured", tracking_path, version_be, backup_time]`.

### 8b. file-history *metadata* — carried on transcript records

The tracked-file bookkeeping is not a separate on-disk file: it rides in the session JSONL as records with `type: "file-history-snapshot"` / `"file-history-delta"` (`artifact_metadata_snapshot`, `adapter.rs:2324-2478`).

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactCheckpointDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    pub(super) timestamp: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) tracked_file_backups: BTreeMap<String, ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactSnapshotDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) is_snapshot_update: bool,
    pub(super) snapshot: ClaudeArtifactCheckpointDocument,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactDeltaDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    pub(super) snapshot_message_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) timestamp: Option<String>,
    pub(super) tracking_path: String,
    pub(super) backup: ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument {
    pub(super) backup_file_name: Value,
    pub(super) version: u64,
    pub(super) backup_time: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) real_parent_dir: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:147-184`)

`backupFileName` is typed `Value` because it is tri-state: a `String` → `ArtifactCapture::ContentExpected` (and its `@v` version must equal `backup.version`, else error), `Null` → `ArtifactCapture::NotCaptured`, anything else → error (`adapter.rs:2403-2426`). Bound `ARTIFACT_METADATA_LIMIT = 512` entries (`adapter.rs:172`). Produces `ArtifactMetadataSnapshotFact` / `ArtifactMetadataEntry` (`facts.rs:2256-2289`).

**Legacy reader:** `read_file_history` (`project_parser.rs:917-968`) walks `file-history/{sessionId}/` with regex `^([0-9a-f]+)@v(\d+)$` (`fingerprint.rs:103-105` — this one *does* accept non-canonical versions like `@v01`), reads content as a UTF-8 string, sorts by file name for cross-engine determinism, → `FileHistorySession { session_id, snapshots: Vec<FileHistorySnapshotFile { hash, version, file_name, content, size }> }` (`types/artifacts.rs:17-34`). `FileBackupEntry { backup_file_name: Option<String>, version: u64, backup_time: String }` at `types/session.rs:241-245`. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_FILE_HISTORY` (`fingerprint.rs:75-76`, `:415`).

## 9. Memory — `project-memory-documents`

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/memory/*.md` → `~/.claude/projects/{projectSlug}/memory/{document}.md`, with `MEMORY.md` flagged as the index
- `PROJECT_MEMORY_STREAM = "project-memory-documents"` (`adapter.rs:125`), `PROJECT_MEMORY_DECODER = "claude-project-memory"` (`adapter.rs:141`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `PROJECT_MEMORY_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:166`; spec `adapter.rs:575-593`)
- Entity scope `Project`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeProjectMemoryContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1482-1507`): exactly 3 components, `components[0]` non-empty, `components[1] == "memory"`; file name must end in `.md`, must not be exactly `".md"`, and must contain no `/` or `\`.

```rust
struct ClaudeProjectMemoryContext {
    project_slug: String,
    native_document_path: String,   // always "memory/{file}"
    is_index: bool,                 // file_name == "MEMORY.md"
}
```

**Record type:** raw UTF-8 Markdown; non-UTF-8 → `preserve_unknown` kind `"project_memory_document"` (`sidecars.rs:1354-1365`).

**Fields** (`decode_project_memory_document`, `sidecars.rs:1345-1404`): no schema. `title` = `first_markdown_heading(&content)` falling back to the file stem (the `.md` strip is an `expect` guaranteed by the context validator, `sidecars.rs:1366-1373`). Document native key = `[project_slug, native_document_path]`.

```rust
pub struct ProjectMemoryDocumentFact {
    pub document: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_project_key: String,
    pub native_document_path: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub content: String,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
    pub is_index: bool,
}
```
(`facts.rs:362-372`)

**Legacy reader:** `read_project_memory` (`project_parser.rs:640-643`) reads **only** `projects/{slug}/memory/MEMORY.md` → `ProjectMemory { project_slug, content }` (`types/project.rs:123-128`). Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_MEMORY`, also MEMORY.md-only (`fingerprint.rs:67-68`, `:540-557`).
