```rust
}
```
(`facts.rs:330-357`; entry doc comment at `:327-329`: *"The entry is metadata about a possible transcript, not proof that the transcript object or any message history is currently available."*)

**Catalog discovery reads the same file independently** (`catalog_discovery.rs:366-403`, `read_index_document`) — bounded stable read with distinct failure modes (`StableRead::Oversized` → "exceeds the catalog document bound"; `Unstable` → "changed during the catalog pass"). Only `entries[]` is walked, and only six fields lifted into:
```rust
struct IndexEntry {
    session_id: String,
    title: Option<String>,
    created: Option<String>,
    modified: Option<String>,
    message_count: Option<u64>,
    project_path: Option<String>,
}
```
(`catalog_discovery.rs:47-54`) — where `title` is `summary` **or else** `firstPrompt` (`:395`), and `sessionId` must pass `is_uuid` or the entry is silently dropped (`:392`). Index evidence introduces transcript-less sessions with `ProjectAssociationBasis::NativeProjectIndex` / `AssociationQuality::NativeClaimed`, and never overwrites the exact `SessionDirectory` association (`:275-317`). The first entry's `projectPath` names the project's `display_path` (`:168-184`).

**Legacy reader:** `read_sessions_index` (`project_parser.rs:495-501`), falling back to `empty_sessions_index()` with `version: 1` on any read or parse failure (`:503-511`):
```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SessionsIndex {
    pub version: u64,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub original_path: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub entries: Vec<SessionIndexEntry>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SessionIndexEntry {
    pub session_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub full_path: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub file_mtime: f64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub first_prompt: String,
    /// `Option` rather than `String` because TS distinguishes two states that
    /// `#[serde(default)]` on a `String` collapses into one: the key **absent**
    /// from the index entry (TS omits it and writes SQL `NULL`) and the key
    /// present but empty (TS writes `''`). The real corpus has both — 6 NULL
    /// and 117 empty — so defaulting to `String` made Rust write `''` for all
    /// 123 and diverge on exactly the 6.
    ///
    /// `skip_serializing_if` keeps the round-trip faithful: a `None` must be
    /// omitted again, not re-emitted as `null`, or `projects.sessions_index`
    /// would diverge instead.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub summary: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub message_count: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub created: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub modified: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub git_branch: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub project_path: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub is_sidechain: bool,
}
```
(`types/project.rs:13-57`) — note **every** field is `#[serde(default)]` here vs required in the adapter, and `file_mtime` is `f64` vs `u64`.

Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_SESSIONS_INDEX` (`fingerprint.rs:69-70`, `:526-534`).

**Prompt normalization** (`src/claude/session_metadata.rs`): `normalize_first_prompt` (`:49-55`) rewrites synthetic index prompts to the literal `"No prompt"`. Synthetic prefixes (`:12-21`, matched case-insensitively on the trimmed value):

```rust
const SYNTHETIC_USER_PREFIXES: &[&str] = &[
    "<local-command-caveat>",

```
`extract_human_prompt` (`:57-75`) rejects records unless `type == "user"` and none of `isMeta`, `isSidechain`, `isCompactSummary`, `isVisibleInTranscriptOnly` is `true`, then truncates to 200 UTF-16 units. `project_session_metadata` (`:77-99`) additionally projects `ai-title` → `aiTitle` and `custom-title` → `customTitle` into:

## 8. file-history — content blobs (`file-history-blobs`)

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include `file-history/*/*@v*` → `~/.claude/file-history/{sessionUuid}/{hash}@v{version}`
- `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_STREAM = "file-history-blobs"` (`adapter.rs:121`), `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_DECODER = "claude-file-history-blob"` (`adapter.rs:137`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:163`; spec `adapter.rs:501-519`)
- Entity scope `Custom("artifact")`, `Canonical`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Scope relation `file-artifacts-from-evidence`, locator `file-history/{native-session-id}/{backup-name}`, identity inputs `["native-session-id","backup-name","artifact-version"]`, primitive `ReplaceDocument` (`adapter.rs:3721-3740`)

**Filename → IDs** (`ClaudeArtifactContentContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1328-1355`): exactly 3 components, `components[0] == "file-history"`, `components[1]` must pass `is_uuid`, `components[2]` parsed by `parse_artifact_file_name`:

```rust
fn parse_artifact_file_name(file_name: &str) -> Option<(String, u64)> {
    let (native_file_hash, version_text) = file_name.rsplit_once("@v")?;
    if native_file_hash.is_empty()
        || !native_file_hash
            .bytes()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&byte))
    {
        return None;
    }
    let version = version_text
        .parse::<u64>()
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| *value > 0)?;
    (version.to_string() == version_text).then(|| (native_file_hash.to_string(), version))
}
```
(`adapter.rs:2719-2733`) — hash must be **lowercase hex only** (uppercase `HASH@v1` rejected, tested `adapter.rs:3648`); version must be canonical and positive (`@v01` rejected, tested `:3652`).

```rust
struct ClaudeArtifactContentContext {
    native_session_id: String,
    native_artifact_id: String,   // the whole "{hash}@v{n}" file name
    native_file_hash: String,
    version: u64,
}
```
(`adapter.rs:1320-1326`; round-trip asserted at `:3817-3825`)

**Record type:** opaque bytes. `decode_artifact_content` (`sidecars.rs:911-960`) performs **no parsing at all** — absent → `IgnoredKnown`; otherwise payload stored verbatim as `Vec<u8>`, base64-encoded on serialization.

**Fields projected:** `native_artifact_id: String`, `native_file_hash: String`, `version: u64` (all from the path), `content: Vec<u8>`, `size_bytes: u64` (`record.payload.len()`), plus `artifact`/`session` legacy keys and `canonical_artifact`/`canonical_session`.

```rust
pub struct ArtifactContentFact {
    pub artifact: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    /// Topology-neutral identities retained in parallel with the legacy
    /// projection keys until the durable artifact schema migrates.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_artifact: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_session: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    pub native_artifact_id: String,
    pub native_file_hash: String,
    pub version: u64,
    #[serde(with = "base64_bytes")]
    pub content: Vec<u8>,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2401-2417`; doc comment `:2398-2400`: *"Its path supplies session, native hash, and version but not a tracked path or run; those relations remain pending until transcript metadata arrives."*)

Artifact native key (`artifact_native_key`, `adapter.rs:2754-2786`): `[session_id, "named-backup", artifact_id]` when a backup name exists, else `[session_id, "not-captured", tracking_path, version_be, backup_time]`.

### 8b. file-history *metadata* — carried on transcript records, not a separate file

The tracked-file bookkeeping rides in the session JSONL as records with `type: "file-history-snapshot"` / `"file-history-delta"` (`artifact_metadata_snapshot`, `adapter.rs:2324-2478`).

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactCheckpointDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    pub(super) timestamp: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) tracked_file_backups: BTreeMap<String, ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactSnapshotDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) is_snapshot_update: bool,
    pub(super) snapshot: ClaudeArtifactCheckpointDocument,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactDeltaDocument {
    pub(super) message_id: String,
    pub(super) snapshot_message_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) timestamp: Option<String>,
    pub(super) tracking_path: String,
    pub(super) backup: ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument {
    pub(super) backup_file_name: Value,
    pub(super) version: u64,
    pub(super) backup_time: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) real_parent_dir: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:147-184`)

**Every field parsed:**

| JSON key | Rust field | Type | Optionality |
|---|---|---|---|
| snapshot: `messageId` | `message_id` | `String` | required; non-empty |
| snapshot: `isSnapshotUpdate` | `is_snapshot_update` | `bool` | `#[serde(default)]` → `false` |
| snapshot: `snapshot` | `snapshot` | `ClaudeArtifactCheckpointDocument` | required |
| checkpoint: `messageId` | `message_id` | `String` | required; non-empty |
| checkpoint: `timestamp` | `timestamp` | `String` | required |
| checkpoint: `trackedFileBackups` | `tracked_file_backups` | `BTreeMap<String, ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument>` | `#[serde(default)]` — keys are tracking paths |
| delta: `messageId` | `message_id` | `String` | required |
| delta: `snapshotMessageId` | `snapshot_message_id` | `String` | required; non-empty |
| delta: `timestamp` | `timestamp` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` |
| delta: `trackingPath` | `tracking_path` | `String` | required; non-empty |
| delta: `backup` | `backup` | `ClaudeArtifactBackupDocument` | required |
| backup: `backupFileName` | `backup_file_name` | `Value` | **tri-state**, see below |
| backup: `version` | `version` | `u64` | required; must be `> 0` |
| backup: `backupTime` | `backup_time` | `String` | required; non-empty |
| backup: `realParentDir` | `real_parent_dir` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` |

`backupFileName` is typed `Value` because it is tri-state (`adapter.rs:2403-2426`): a `String` → `ArtifactCapture::ContentExpected`, and its parsed `@v` version must equal `backup.version` or the record errors ("backup version {} disagrees with {file_name}"); `Value::Null` → `ArtifactCapture::NotCaptured`; anything else → "backup file name must be a string or null". Bound `ARTIFACT_METADATA_LIMIT = 512` entries (`adapter.rs:172`). Duplicate artifact keys within one observation are rejected twice — legacy ("maps multiple paths to one artifact") and canonical ("repeats a canonical artifact identity").

```rust
pub struct ArtifactMetadataEntry {
    pub artifact: EntityKey,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_artifact: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    pub native_artifact_id: Option<String>,
    pub tracking_path: String,
    pub real_parent_dir: Option<String>,
    pub version: u64,
    pub backup_time: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub capture: ArtifactCapture,
}

pub struct ArtifactMetadataSnapshotFact {
    pub session: EntityKey,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub canonical_session: Option<CanonicalEntityKey>,
    pub native_message_id: String,
    pub native_snapshot_message_id: String,
    pub observation_kind: ArtifactObservationKind,
    pub is_snapshot_update: bool,
    pub source_time: Option<QualifiedTimestamp>,
    pub artifacts: Vec<ArtifactMetadataEntry>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2256-2289`; `ArtifactObservationKind {Checkpoint, Delta}` at `:2241-2245`, `ArtifactCapture {ContentExpected, NotCaptured}` at `:2250-2254` — doc comment `:2247-2249`: *"A `NotCaptured` artifact is positive native evidence that a path was newly created without a backup; it is distinct from a named blob that has not arrived yet."*)

**Legacy reader:** `read_file_history` (`project_parser.rs:917-968`) walks `file-history/{sessionId}/` with regex `^([0-9a-f]+)@v(\d+)$` (`fingerprint.rs:103-105`), reads content as a UTF-8 string, and sorts by file name for cross-engine determinism (the comment at `:952-960` notes TS came out sorted in all 54 macOS-corpus sessions and Rust in only 4, so every multi-snapshot session diverged until the sort landed):
```rust
pub struct FileHistorySession {
    pub session_id: String,
    #[serde(default)] pub snapshots: Vec<FileHistorySnapshotFile>,
}

pub struct FileHistorySnapshotFile {
    pub hash: String,
    pub version: u64,
    pub file_name: String,
    pub content: String,
    #[serde(default)] pub size: u64,
}
```
(`types/artifacts.rs:17-34`)
```rust
pub struct FileBackupEntry {
    pub backup_file_name: Option<String>,
    pub version: u64,
    pub backup_time: String,
}
```

(`types/session.rs:241-245`) — the legacy shape uses `Option<String>` where the adapter uses tri-state `Value`, and `tracked_file_backups` there is a `std::collections::HashMap<String, FileBackupEntry>` (`types/session.rs:236`) vs the adapter's `BTreeMap`.

Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_FILE_HISTORY` (`fingerprint.rs:75-76`, `:415`).

## 9. Memory — `project-memory-documents` (incl. `MEMORY.md`)

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/memory/*.md` → `~/.claude/projects/{projectSlug}/memory/{document}.md`
- `PROJECT_MEMORY_STREAM = "project-memory-documents"` (`adapter.rs:125`), `PROJECT_MEMORY_DECODER = "claude-project-memory"` (`adapter.rs:141`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `PROJECT_MEMORY_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:166`; spec `adapter.rs:575-593`)
- Entity scope `Project`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeProjectMemoryContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1482-1507`): exactly 3 components, `components[0]` non-empty, `components[1] == "memory"`; the file name must end in `.md`, must not be exactly `".md"`, and must contain no `/` or `\`. Negative cases in tests (`adapter.rs:4241-4242`): bare `memory/MEMORY.md`, `project/MEMORY.md`.

```rust
struct ClaudeProjectMemoryContext {
    project_slug: String,
    native_document_path: String,   // always "memory/{file}"
    is_index: bool,                 // file_name == "MEMORY.md"
}
```
(`adapter.rs:1396-1401`; `is_index` set at `:1505`)

**Record type:** raw UTF-8 Markdown; non-UTF-8 → `preserve_unknown` kind `"project_memory_document"` (`sidecars.rs:1354-1365`). Absent → `IgnoredKnown`.

**Fields projected** (`decode_project_memory_document`, `sidecars.rs:1345-1404`): no schema.

| Output field | Type | Derivation |
|---|---|---|
| `native_project_key` | `String` | `project_slug` from path |
| `native_document_path` | `String` | `"memory/{file}"` |
| `title` | `String` | `first_markdown_heading(&content)` else the `.md`-stripped file stem (`sidecars.rs:1366-1373`; both strips are `expect`s guaranteed by the context validator) |
| `content` | `String` | whole UTF-8 body |
| `size_bytes` | `u64` | `record.payload.len()` |
| `is_index` | `bool` | `file_name == "MEMORY.md"` |

Document native key = `[project_slug, native_document_path]` (`sidecars.rs:1374-1379`).

```rust
pub struct ProjectMemoryDocumentFact {
    pub document: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_project_key: String,
    pub native_document_path: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub content: String,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
    pub is_index: bool,
}
```
(`facts.rs:362-372`; doc comment `:359-361`: *"An adapter may mark a native index document, while links and other embedded references remain content rather than asserted entity relationships."*)

**Legacy reader:** `read_project_memory` (`project_parser.rs:640-643`) reads **only** `projects/{slug}/memory/MEMORY.md`, returning `Option<String>`:
```rust
pub struct ProjectMemory {
    pub project_slug: String,
    pub content: String,
}
```
(`types/project.rs:123-128`). Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_MEMORY`, also MEMORY.md-only (`fingerprint.rs:67-68`, `:540-557`) — so a non-index memory doc is visible to the adapter but invisible to warm start.

## 10. Settings — `interpretation-settings` (`settings.json` + `settings.local.json`; managed = absent)

**Path / glob:** root `home`, include **exactly** `["settings.json", "settings.local.json"]` → `~/.claude/settings.json`, `~/.claude/settings.local.json` (`adapter.rs:618-624`)
- `INTERPRETATION_SETTINGS_STREAM = "interpretation-settings"` (`adapter.rs:127`), `INTERPRETATION_SETTINGS_DECODER = "claude-interpretation-settings"` (`adapter.rs:143`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `INTERPRETATION_SETTINGS_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:168`; spec `adapter.rs:613-634`)
- Entity scope `Instance`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`
- The include list is asserted verbatim in tests: `vec!["settings.json", "settings.local.json"]` (`adapter.rs:3376`)

**Filename → layer** (`ClaudeInterpretationSettingsContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1417-1440`): must be a **single** root-level component; `"settings.json" → InterpretationSettingsLayer::Global`, `"settings.local.json" → Local`, anything else is a hard `path_error`. Rejected in tests (`adapter.rs:4320-4323`): `settings.json.bak`, `project/settings.json`, **`managed-settings.json`**.

```rust
struct ClaudeInterpretationSettingsContext {
    layer: InterpretationSettingsLayer,
    native_document_path: String,
}
```
(`adapter.rs:1411-1415`)

### Managed settings: confirmed ABSENT by design

`managed-settings.json` appears **only** as a rejected path in a negative test (`adapter.rs:4323`). The exclusion is documented policy, not an oversight — `facts.rs:392-396`:

```rust
/// The precedence layer represented by one interpretation-settings document.
///
/// The common pack deliberately models only the source instance's global and
/// local documents. Managed policy, command-line overrides, and project roots
/// outside the configured source instance are not inferred.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum InterpretationSettingsLayer {
    Global,
    Local,
}
```
(`facts.rs:392-402`)

**Record type:** a JSON object, decoded by an **allowlist** (`decode_interpretation_settings_snapshot`, `sidecars.rs:1549-1628`), not by serde derive. Root must be an object ("document root must be an object"); `permissions` must be an object if present ("permissions must be an object").

**Every field parsed:**

| JSON key | Rust field | Type | Reader fn | Location |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `agent` | `agent` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` | `sidecars.rs:1576` |
| `model` | `model` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` | `:1577` |
| `effortLevel` | `effort_level` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` | `:1578` |
| `plansDirectory` | `plans_directory` | `Option<String>` | `optional_settings_string` | `:1579` |
| `alwaysThinkingEnabled` | `always_thinking_enabled` | `Option<bool>` | `optional_settings_bool` | `:1580-1584` |
| `autoCompactEnabled` | `auto_compact_enabled` | `Option<bool>` | `optional_settings_bool` | `:1585-1589` |
| `skipAutoPermissionPrompt` | `skip_auto_permission_prompt` | `Option<bool>` | `optional_settings_bool` | `:1590-1594` |
| `permissions.defaultMode` | `permission_default_mode` | `Option<String>` | `optional_nested_settings_string` | `:1595-1599` |
| `permissions.disableBypassPermissionsMode` | `disable_bypass_permissions_mode` | `Option<String>` | `optional_nested_settings_string` | `:1600-1604` |
| `permissions.disableAutoMode` | `disable_auto_mode` | `Option<String>` | `optional_nested_settings_string` | `:1605-1609` |
| `permissions.allow` | `permission_allow` | `Option<Vec<String>>` | `optional_nested_settings_string_array` | `:1610-1614` |
| `permissions.ask` | `permission_ask` | `Option<Vec<String>>` | `optional_nested_settings_string_array` | `:1615-1619` |
| `permissions.deny` | `permission_deny` | `Option<Vec<String>>` | `optional_nested_settings_string_array` | `:1620-1624` |
| `enabledPlugins` | `enabled_plugins` | `Option<BTreeMap<String, bool>>` | `optional_settings_bool_map` | `:1625` |
| `hooks` | `hook_events` | `Option<BTreeMap<String, HookEventSummary>>` | `optional_hook_event_summaries` | `:1626` |

```rust
/// Allowlisted settings that materially affect source interpretation.
///
/// Arbitrary native keys, environment values, hook bodies, status-line
/// commands, marketplace locations, and UI preferences are intentionally not
/// represented. Optional collections distinguish an absent field from an
/// explicitly present empty collection so scope merging stays truthful.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct InterpretationSettingsSnapshot {
    pub agent: Option<String>,
    pub model: Option<String>,
    pub effort_level: Option<String>,
    pub plans_directory: Option<String>,
    pub always_thinking_enabled: Option<bool>,
    pub auto_compact_enabled: Option<bool>,
    pub skip_auto_permission_prompt: Option<bool>,
    pub permission_default_mode: Option<String>,
    pub disable_bypass_permissions_mode: Option<String>,
    pub disable_auto_mode: Option<String>,
    pub permission_allow: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub permission_ask: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub permission_deny: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub enabled_plugins: Option<BTreeMap<String, bool>>,
    pub hook_events: Option<BTreeMap<String, HookEventSummary>>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:420-443`)

**Reader helpers** — each rejects a wrong JSON type with a shape failure:
- `optional_settings_string` (`sidecars.rs:1630-1645`): `None` → `Ok(None)`; `Value::String` → validated + cloned; anything else → "{field} must be a string"
- `optional_nested_settings_string` (`:1647-1656`): returns `Ok(None)` when the whole `permissions` object is absent
- `optional_settings_bool` (`:1658-1670`): `Value::Bool` only, else "{field} must be a boolean"
- `optional_nested_settings_string_array` (`:1672-1707`): must be `Value::Array`; length ≤ `SETTINGS_COLLECTION_LIMIT`; every element must be `Value::String` else "{field}[{index}] must be a string"
- `optional_settings_bool_map` (`:1709-1739`): must be `Value::Object`; size ≤ limit; every value must be `Value::Bool` else "enabledPlugins.{plugin} must be a boolean"; every key validated as a settings string
- `validate_settings_string` (`:1804-1814`): `value.len() > SETTINGS_STRING_MAX_BYTES` → bounds failure

### Redaction / privacy notes

**Hooks collapse to counts only.** `optional_hook_event_summaries` (`sidecars.rs:1741-1802`) walks `hooks` as `event → Array<matcher>`, requires each matcher to be an object carrying a `hooks` array, and retains **nothing but two integers per event**. No command, prompt, URL, matcher pattern, script path, or agent name survives:

```rust
/// Redacted hook metadata retained without command, prompt, URL, or agent
/// bodies. Counts describe declarations, not successful hook executions.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct HookEventSummary {
    pub declared_matcher_count: u64,
    pub declared_hook_count: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:412-418`)

Construction site (`sidecars.rs:1793-1799`):
```rust
summaries.insert(
    event.clone(),
    HookEventSummary {
        declared_matcher_count: matchers.len() as u64,
        declared_hook_count: hook_count as u64,
    },
);
```
Bounds enforced along the way: `hooks` must be an object; ≤ 4_096 events; ≤ 4_096 matchers per event; the running `hook_count` uses `checked_add` and must stay ≤ 4_096 ("hooks.{event} hook count exceeds platform limits" / "exceeds 4096 hooks").

**Plugins reduce to a name→bool map.** `enabledPlugins` is the *only* plugin-related data captured anywhere in the crate — `Option<BTreeMap<String, bool>>` via `optional_settings_bool_map` (`sidecars.rs:1709-1739`). No plugin manifest, path, version, marketplace, or component is read.

**Everything else in settings is deliberately dropped.** Per `facts.rs:420-425` (quoted verbatim above): arbitrary native keys, environment values, hook bodies, status-line commands, marketplace locations, and UI preferences are intentionally not represented. Skills appear nowhere in the allowlist.

**Bounds & failure codes:**
- `SETTINGS_COLLECTION_LIMIT = 4_096` (`adapter.rs:174`)
- `SETTINGS_STRING_MAX_BYTES = 16 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:175`)
```rust
#[derive(Debug)]
pub(super) struct InterpretationSettingsDecodeFailure {
    pub(super) code: &'static str,
    pub(super) message: String,
}

impl InterpretationSettingsDecodeFailure {
    fn shape(message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self { code: "claude_settings_invalid_shape", message: message.into() }
    }

    fn bounds(message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self { code: "claude_settings_bounds", message: message.into() }
    }
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:1527-1547`) plus `"claude_settings_invalid_json"` carrying serde's line and column (`:1552-1560`).

**Invalid documents still emit a fact** (`decode_interpretation_settings`, `sidecars.rs:1465-1525`) with `document_status: Invalid`, `settings: None`, `error_code: Some(code)`, disposition `PreservedUnknown`, plus an `AdapterDiagnostic { class: AdapterErrorClass::RecordPermanent, code, message }`. Rationale at `facts.rs:445-450`:

```rust
/// One independently replaceable interpretation-settings document.
///
/// Invalid documents retain only redacted health evidence. They never become
/// an empty or last-known-good settings layer, which prevents stale permission
/// state from being silently presented as current.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct InterpretationSettingsFact {
    pub document: EntityKey,
    pub scope: EntityKey,
    pub layer: InterpretationSettingsLayer,
    pub native_document_path: String,
    pub document_status: InterpretationSettingsDocumentStatus,
    pub settings: Option<InterpretationSettingsSnapshot>,
    pub error_code: Option<String>,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:445-460`; `InterpretationSettingsDocumentStatus {Valid, Invalid}` at `:404-410`)

Document entity key = `native_document_path` bytes; scope entity key is the constant `b"root"` (`sidecars.rs:1506-1515`).

**Support probe** also reads `settings.json` (`support_probe.rs:21-30`, `inspect_settings`), bounded by `MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`:17`), and checks only that it parses to a JSON **object** → marker `"settings.schema-shape"` (`:151`).

## 11. Workflows — runs (`workflow-runs`, `workflows/wf_*.json`)

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/workflows/wf_*.json` → `~/.claude/projects/{slug}/{sessionUuid}/workflows/wf_{id}.json`
- `WORKFLOW_RUN_STREAM = "workflow-runs"` (`adapter.rs:122`), `WORKFLOW_RUN_DECODER = "claude-workflow-run"` (`adapter.rs:138`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `WORKFLOW_RUN_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:164`; spec `adapter.rs:520-538`)
- Entity scope `Custom("workflow")`, `Canonical`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Locator `{project-key}/{native-session-id}/workflows`, `relative_selector: Some("wf_*.json")`, identity inputs `["project-key","native-session-id","observed-workflow-id"]` (`adapter.rs:3924-3932`)

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeWorkflowContext::run_from_path`, `adapter.rs:1515-1532`, then `validate`, `:1554-1580`): exactly 4 components, `components[2] == "workflows"`, strip `.json` from `components[3]`. Then `validate` requires: project slug non-empty; session passes `is_uuid`; workflow id **starts with `wf_` and has a non-empty suffix** (`wf_` alone rejected, tested `adapter.rs:4089`).

```rust
struct ClaudeWorkflowContext {
    project_slug: String,
    native_session_id: String,
    native_workflow_id: String,
}
```
(`adapter.rs:1362-1367`)

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeWorkflowRunDocument {
    pub(super) run_id: String,
    pub(super) timestamp: String,
    pub(super) task_id: String,
    pub(super) script: String,
    pub(super) script_path: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) args: Option<String>,
    pub(super) agent_count: u64,
    pub(super) duration_ms: u64,
    pub(super) summary: String,
    pub(super) workflow_name: String,
    pub(super) status: String,
    pub(super) start_time: u64,
    pub(super) default_model: String,
    pub(super) total_tokens: u64,
    pub(super) total_tool_calls: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) error: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:186-207`)

**Every field parsed:**

| JSON key | Rust field | Type | Optionality / validation |
|---|---|---|---|
| `runId` | `run_id` | `String` | required; **must equal the filename workflow id** |
| `timestamp` | `timestamp` | `String` | required; non-blank; becomes `finished_at` verbatim |
| `taskId` | `task_id` | `String` | required; non-blank |
| `script` | `script` | `String` | required; non-blank |
| `scriptPath` | `script_path` | `String` | required; non-blank |
| `args` | `args` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` — one of only two optionals |
| `agentCount` | `agent_count` | `u64` | required |
| `durationMs` | `duration_ms` | `u64` | required |
| `summary` | `summary` | `String` | required; non-blank |
| `workflowName` | `workflow_name` | `String` | required; non-blank; becomes `name` |
| `status` | `status` | `String` | required; non-blank |
| `startTime` | `start_time` | `u64` | required; epoch-ms; must fit `i64` |
| `defaultModel` | `default_model` | `String` | required; non-blank |
| `totalTokens` | `total_tokens` | `u64` | required |
| `totalToolCalls` | `total_tool_calls` | `u64` | required |
| `error` | `error` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` |

**Decode** (`decode_workflow_run`, `sidecars.rs:962-1067`), gates via `preserve_workflow_contract_loss` with kind `"workflow_run"` (`:1830-1843`):
- absent → `IgnoredKnown`
- not valid JSON → "is not valid JSON: {error}"; shape mismatch → "is not a supported run document: {error}"
- `runId != context.native_workflow_id` → "payload runId does not match the source file name"
- the eight blank checks are an explicit loop (`sidecars.rs:1002-1020`) over `taskId`, `workflowName`, `status`, `defaultModel`, `script`, `scriptPath`, `summary`, `timestamp` → "has an empty {field}"
- `i64::try_from(start_time)` failure → "startTime exceeds the supported epoch-millisecond range"
- **Asymmetric timestamps:** `started_at` = `epoch_millis_timestamp(start_time)` (epoch-ms → ISO); `finished_at` = `native_timestamp(&document.timestamp)` (verbatim). The `timestamp` field is treated as the completion time.
- `status` normalized by `workflow_status` (`adapter.rs:2808-2817`):
```rust
fn workflow_status(native_status: &str) -> WorkflowStatus {
    match native_status {
        "pending" | "queued" => WorkflowStatus::Pending,
        "running" | "in_progress" => WorkflowStatus::Running,
        "completed" => WorkflowStatus::Succeeded,
        "failed" => WorkflowStatus::Failed,
        "cancelled" | "canceled" | "killed" => WorkflowStatus::Cancelled,
        other => WorkflowStatus::Other(other.to_string()),
    }
}
```
- The entire raw document is retained as `native_snapshot: Value` (`sidecars.rs:971-981`, assigned `:1063`)

Workflow native key = `[session_id, workflow_id]` (`workflow_native_key`, `adapter.rs:2788-2793`; `workflow_key`, `:2795-2806`).

```rust
pub struct WorkflowSnapshotFact {
    pub workflow: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_workflow_id: String,
    pub native_task_id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub native_status: String,
    pub status: WorkflowStatus,
    pub default_model: String,
    pub script: String,
    pub script_path: String,
    pub args: Option<String>,
    pub summary: String,
    pub error: Option<String>,
    pub started_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub finished_at: QualifiedTimestamp,
    pub duration_ms: u64,
    pub agent_count: u64,
    pub total_tokens: u64,
    pub total_tool_calls: u64,
    pub native_snapshot: Value,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2473-2499`; `WorkflowStatus {Pending, Running, Succeeded, Failed, Cancelled, Other(String)}` at `:2461-2471`, whose doc note reads *"This status applies only to the workflow orchestration record; it is never copied onto member child runs."*)

**Legacy reader:** `read_workflows` (`project_parser.rs:764-822`) is far looser — accepts any `wf_*.json`; falls back to the filename stem when `runId` is absent; falls back to `workflow_id` when `workflowName` is absent; `status` defaults to `""`; all counters read as `f64` via `data.get(k).and_then(as_f64).unwrap_or(0.0)`; additionally computes `subagent_count` by counting `subagents/workflows/{wf}/agent-*.jsonl` (`count_workflow_subagents`, `:823-841`); sorted by `workflow_id`:
```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct WorkflowRun {
    pub workflow_id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub agent_count: f64,
    pub total_tokens: f64,
    pub total_tool_calls: f64,
    pub duration_ms: f64,
    pub subagent_count: f64,
    /// The full raw `{runId}.json` run record.
    pub data: Value,
    /// Parsed `journal.jsonl` entries (started/result events).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub journal: Vec<Value>,
}
```
(`types/workflow.rs:11-27`)

## 12. Workflows — journal (`workflow-journals`, `journal.jsonl`)

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/subagents/workflows/*/journal.jsonl` → `~/.claude/projects/{slug}/{sessionUuid}/subagents/workflows/wf_{id}/journal.jsonl`
- `WORKFLOW_JOURNAL_STREAM = "workflow-journals"` (`adapter.rs:123`), `WORKFLOW_JOURNAL_DECODER = "claude-workflow-journal"` (`adapter.rs:139`)
- Driver **`AppendDelimited(AppendDelimitedConfig::json_lines())`** with `ConsistencyPolicy::IncrementalCursor` — the **only non-transcript append stream** (`adapter.rs:539-555`)
- Entity scope `Custom("workflow_member")`, `Canonical`, `Interactive`
- Locator `{project-key}/{native-session-id}/subagents/workflows/{observed-workflow-id}/journal.jsonl` (`adapter.rs:3933-3941`)

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeWorkflowContext::journal_from_path`, `adapter.rs:1534-1552`): exactly 6 components with `components[2] == "subagents"`, `components[3] == "workflows"`, `components[5] == "journal.jsonl"`; workflow id is `components[4]`; then the same `validate` (non-empty slug, UUID session, `wf_` prefix). A shallower `project/{session}/workflows/wf_main/journal.jsonl` is rejected (tested `adapter.rs:4164`).

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeWorkflowJournalDocument {
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub(super) kind: String,
    pub(super) agent_id: String,
    pub(super) key: String,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:209-216`)

**Every field parsed (per JSONL line):**

| JSON key | Rust field | Type | Optionality |
|---|---|---|---|
| `type` | `kind` | `String` | required (`#[serde(rename = "type")]`) |
| `agentId` | `agent_id` | `String` | required; non-empty after trim |
| `key` | `key` | `String` | required; non-empty after trim → `native_event_key` |
| `result` | — | `Option<Value>` | **not in the struct**; read from the raw `Value` (`sidecars.rs:1117`, `:1121`) |

**Decode** (`decode_workflow_journal`, `sidecars.rs:1069-1219`) — the `type`/`result` pairing is strict (`:1116-1145`):
```rust
let (kind, result) = match document.kind.as_str() {
    "started" if value.get("result").is_none() => (WorkflowMemberEventKind::Started, None),
    "result" => {
        let Some(result) = value.get("result").cloned() else { /* contract loss */ };
        (WorkflowMemberEventKind::Result, Some(result))
    }
    "started" => { /* contract loss: "started record unexpectedly contains a result value" */ }
    _ => { /* contract loss: "record has an unsupported event type" */ }
};
```
Other gates: absent → `IgnoredKnown`; invalid JSON → "record is not valid JSON"; shape mismatch → "record is not supported"; empty `agentId` → "record has an empty agentId"; empty `key` → "record has an empty key". All via `preserve_workflow_contract_loss` with kind `"workflow_journal"`.

Keys: member native key = `workflow_native_key(context) ++ agent_id` (`sidecars.rs:1148-1149`); child-run native key = `format!("{}\0{}\0{}", session_id, workflow_id, agent_id)` (`:1150-1153`); affiliation native key = `["workflow", child_run_native_key, workflow_native_key]` (`:1161-1164`).

Also emits an `ActorAffiliationRevisionFact` with `dimension: ActorAffiliationDimension::Workflow`, `state: ActorAffiliationState::Present`, `member: Some(canonical_member)`, `effective_at: None` (`sidecars.rs:1202-1217`).

```rust
pub struct WorkflowMemberEventFact {
    pub workflow: EntityKey,
    pub member: EntityKey,
    pub child_run: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_workflow_id: String,
    pub native_agent_id: String,
    pub native_event_key: String,
    pub kind: WorkflowMemberEventKind,
    pub result: Option<Value>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2507-2522`; `WorkflowMemberEventKind {Started, Result}` at `:2501-2505`; doc comment `:2507-2509`: *"It proves membership and native start/result observation, but a result does not by itself classify the child run as succeeded or failed."*)

**Legacy reader:** `read_workflow_journal` (`project_parser.rs:843-858`) collects every parseable line as a raw `Value` into `WorkflowRun.journal` — no type discrimination at all. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_WORKFLOW_JOURNAL` (`fingerprint.rs:81-82`, `:644-652`).

## 13. Persisted tool results (`.txt`) — `persisted-tool-results`

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/tool-results/*.txt` → `~/.claude/projects/{slug}/{sessionUuid}/tool-results/{toolUseId}.txt`
- `PERSISTED_TOOL_RESULT_STREAM = "persisted-tool-results"` (`adapter.rs:126`), `PERSISTED_TOOL_RESULT_DECODER = "claude-persisted-tool-result"` (`adapter.rs:142`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `PERSISTED_TOOL_RESULT_MAX_BYTES = 16 * 1024 * 1024` — **the largest bound in the adapter**, 16× the next largest (`adapter.rs:167`; spec `adapter.rs:594-612`)
- Entity scope `Custom("persisted_tool_result")`, authority **`Supplemental`**, `Interactive`

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudePersistedToolResultContext::from_path`, `adapter.rs:1447-1475`): exactly 4 components, `components[0]` non-empty, `components[1]` must pass `is_uuid`, `components[2] == "tool-results"`; strip `.txt` from `components[3]` → `native_tool_use_id` (non-empty). `native_document_path` is rebuilt as `format!("tool-results/{}", components[3])`.

```rust
struct ClaudePersistedToolResultContext {
    project_slug: String,
    native_session_id: String,
    native_tool_use_id: String,
    native_document_path: String,
}
```
(`adapter.rs:1403-1409`)

**Record type:** raw UTF-8 text; non-UTF-8 → `preserve_unknown` kind `"persisted_tool_result"` (`sidecars.rs:1415-1426`). Absent → `IgnoredKnown`. No field parsing whatsoever.

**Fields projected** (`decode_persisted_tool_result`, `sidecars.rs:1406-1463`):

| Output field | Type | Derivation |
|---|---|---|
| `native_project_key` | `String` | path component 0 |
| `native_session_id` | `String` | path component 1 (UUID) |
| `native_tool_use_id` | `String` | `.txt`-stripped file name |
| `native_document_path` | `String` | `"tool-results/{file}"` |
| `content` | `String` | whole UTF-8 body |
| `size_bytes` | `u64` | `record.payload.len()` |

Result native key = `[session_id, tool_use_id]` (`sidecars.rs:1427-1432`).

```rust
/// One independently replaceable persisted tool-result text document.
///
/// The native file may supplement a transcript tool call or inline result,
/// but its presence alone is not message or run evidence. Correlation is
/// performed by the common projector using the session-scoped native tool ID.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PersistedToolResultFact {
    pub result: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub project: EntityKey,
    pub native_project_key: String,
    pub native_session_id: String,
    pub native_tool_use_id: String,
    pub native_document_path: String,
    pub content: String,
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}
```
(`facts.rs:374-390`)

**Legacy reader:** `read_tool_results` (`project_parser.rs:888-913`) — same `.txt` suffix rule, `tool_use_id = name.trim_end_matches(".txt")`:
```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PersistedToolResult {
    pub tool_use_id: String,
    pub content: String,
}
```
(`types/project.rs:112-117`). Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_TOOL_RESULT` (`fingerprint.rs:65-66`, `:711-748`).

## 14. Subagent metadata sidecar — `subagent-metadata`

The 14th non-transcript stream: a JSON sidecar beside each subagent JSONL.

**Path / glob:** root `projects`, include `*/*/subagents/**/agent-*.meta.json`
- `SUBAGENT_META_STREAM = "subagent-metadata"` (`adapter.rs:114`), `SUBAGENT_META_DECODER = "claude-subagent-metadata"` (`adapter.rs:130`)
- `ReplaceDocument`, `SUBAGENT_META_MAX_BYTES = 64 * 1024` (`adapter.rs:156`; spec `adapter.rs:367-385`)
- Entity scope `Run`, authority `Supplemental`, `ForegroundRepair`
- Locator `{actor-transcript-object}.meta.json`, identity inputs `["actor-transcript-object"]` (`adapter.rs:3851-3859`)
- Schema version `"claude-code-subagent-meta-v1"` (`adapter.rs:230`)

**Path → IDs** (`ClaudeTranscriptContext::subagent_meta` → `subagent_object(path, ".meta.json")`, `adapter.rs:1058-1087`): ≥ 4 components with `components[2] == "subagents"`; file name must be `agent-{id}.meta.json` with non-empty id; `workflow_id` is discovered by scanning component pairs for `("workflows", next_non_empty)`:
```rust
let workflow_id = components.windows(2).find_map(|pair| {
    (pair[0] == "workflows" && !pair[1].is_empty()).then(|| pair[1].clone())
});
```
(`adapter.rs:1078-1080`)

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct ClaudeTranscriptContext {
    project_slug: String,
    session_id: String,
    agent_id: Option<String>,
    workflow_id: Option<String>,
}
```
(`adapter.rs:1022-1028`), wrapped as
```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct ClaudeSubagentMetadataContext {
    child: ClaudeTranscriptContext,
}
```
(`adapter.rs:1106-1109`). Run native key (`run_native_key`, `:1093-1103`): `format!("{}\0{}\0{}", session_id, workflow_id.unwrap_or(""), agent_id)` for subagents; bare `session_id` for a root transcript.

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub(super) struct ClaudeSubagentMetadataDocument {
    pub(super) agent_type: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) description: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) name: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) team_name: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) spawn_depth: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) worktree_path: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub(super) tool_use_id: Option<String>,
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:13-29`)

**Every field parsed:**

| JSON key | Rust field | Type | Optionality |
|---|---|---|---|
| `agentType` | `agent_type` | `String` | required; non-empty else `PreservedUnknown` |
| `description` | `description` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` |
| `name` | `name` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` — doubles as team member name |
| `teamName` | `team_name` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` |
| `spawnDepth` | `spawn_depth` | `Option<u32>` | `#[serde(default)]` |
| `worktreePath` | `worktree_path` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` |
| `toolUseId` | `tool_use_id` | `Option<String>` | `#[serde(default)]` → projected as `native_task_id` |

**Decode** (`decode_subagent_metadata`, `adapter.rs:1718-1793`): emits `DelegationMetadataFact`; and when **both** `teamName` and `name` are non-empty, additionally emits a `Team`-dimension `ActorAffiliationRevisionFact` for the child run via `ClaudeTeamAffiliationActor::Child` (`adapter.rs:1777-1791`). This is the **second on-disk source of team membership** besides `teams/*/config.json`. A missing `agent_id` in the context is an `invalid_contract` error, not a contract loss.

```rust
pub struct DelegationMetadataFact {
    pub child_run: EntityKey,
    pub session: EntityKey,
    pub native_child_id: String,
    pub agent_type: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub spawn_depth: Option<u32>,
    pub worktree_path: Option<String>,
    pub native_task_id: Option<String>,
}
```
(`facts.rs:2056-2066`)

**Legacy reader:** `read_one_subagent` (`project_parser.rs:747-750`) opens the sibling via `path.with_extension("meta.json")`:
```rust
/// `agent-{id}.meta.json` — TS `SubagentMeta`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SubagentMeta {
    pub agent_type: String,
    // Absent on nested workflow metas — must be optional or their parse fails.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub description: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub name: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub spawn_depth: Option<f64>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub worktree_path: Option<String>,
}
```
(`types/project.rs:92-106`) — **no `team_name`, no `tool_use_id`**, and `spawn_depth` is `f64` vs the adapter's `u32`. Fingerprinted `CATEGORY_SUBAGENT_META` (`fingerprint.rs:79-80`, `:699-708`); the comment at `:659-663` explains it gets its own category because a sidecar can change without the transcript growing.

## 15. Catalog discovery — the projects tree as a stream

`src/claude/catalog_discovery.rs` derives the catalog from filesystem layout with **no transcript bytes required** (module doc `:1-25`):

```text
<projects>/<project-slug>/                     -> a project
<projects>/<project-slug>/<uuid>.jsonl         -> a transcript-backed session
<projects>/<project-slug>/<uuid>/              -> a session with side data
                                                  (subagents, workflows, tool
                                                  results) and possibly no
                                                  transcript of its own
<projects>/<project-slug>/sessions-index.json  -> sessions the agent knows about,
                                                  including ones with no transcript
```

Constants: `PROJECTS_ROOT = "projects"`, `SESSION_INDEX_NAME = "sessions-index.json"` (`:43-44`).

- Scan is pinned to `max_depth: 2` regardless of `limits` (`:69-77`) — *"Claude's projects root is exactly two levels deep… so the scan depth is pinned rather than taken from `limits`."*
- `catalog_selection` (`:250-270`): depth-1 dirs → `Recurse`; depth-2 dirs whose name is a UUID → `Include` (recorded, **never descended into**, so a deep `subagents/` or `workflows/` tree costs nothing); depth-2 files named `sessions-index.json` or ending `.jsonl` → `Include`; everything else `Ignore`
- A third of real-machine projects are discoverable **only** through the session-directory form (`:17-18`)
- With no session index, `display_path` comes from **one bounded transcript-head read** (`read_transcript_cwd`, `:212-219`): splits the prefix on `\n`, parses each complete line, takes the first record's `cwd`; the trailing partial line is ignored. Candidate = newest by `(modified_ns, relative_path)` (`:141-155`), because *"The slug cannot be reversed safely because '-' may be either a path separator or part of a directory name"* (`:186-191`)
- `insert_session` (`:326-364`) merges agreeing evidence (a session appears twice inside one project — as a directory and as a transcript file) and records the loser as an explicit `DiscoveredAssociationConflict` when projects disagree
- `is_uuid` here is a **separate implementation** from the adapter's, same semantics (`:425-433` vs `adapter.rs:2864-2872`)
- `TranscriptHeadCandidate { relative_path: String, modified_ns: i128 }` (`:59-62`); `modified_ms` truncates ns→ms saturating at `i64::MAX` (`:421-423`)

---

## Confirmed ABSENT — not parsed anywhere in the crate

Verified by repo-wide grep over `crates/spaghetti-napi/src/`:

| Stream | Status & citation |
|---|---|
| **`~/.claude.json`** | **Zero occurrences** anywhere in `src/`. No reader, no stream, no test. |
| **`~/.claude/history.jsonl`** | **Not parsed.** The only `history.jsonl` matches in the tree belong to a different adapter or to synthetic fixtures: `src/grok/adapter.rs:234,251` and `src/grok/reader.rs:25` (Grok's `chat_history.jsonl`), `src/grok/catalog_discovery.rs:33`, `src/source/access.rs:1385,1419` (test literal `b"session/history.jsonl"`), and `src/adapter/scope.rs:961,977` — the last being a hand-built `ScopeObservationSourceBinding { stream_id: "history-documents", source_pattern: "**/history.jsonl" }` inside a manifest-validation unit test, not a Claude stream. |
| **`shell-snapshots/`** | **Zero occurrences.** Not fingerprinted, not selected, not decoded. |
| **Managed / policy settings** | **Deliberately excluded.** `managed-settings.json` appears only as a rejected path in a negative test (`adapter.rs:4323`). Policy documented at `facts.rs:392-396`: *"The common pack deliberately models only the source instance's global and local documents. Managed policy, command-line overrides, and project roots outside the configured source instance are not inferred."* |
| **`CLAUDE.md` discovery** | **Zero occurrences of `CLAUDE.md`** in `src/`. Project memory is `projects/*/memory/*.md` only (§9); there is no walk of a working directory, no ancestor search, no user-level memory file. |
| **`plugins/`** | **Not scanned.** The only plugin data anywhere is the `enabledPlugins` name→bool map inside settings (§10). `fingerprint.rs:406-408` names the skipped dirs explicitly: *"A `~/.claude` tree contains unrelated dirs (`plugins/`, `shell/`, `ide/`, `statsig/`, etc.) we do *not* want to fingerprint. An allow-listed walk is O(files we care about), not O(everything)."* The allow-list is `discover_all` (`fingerprint.rs:409-419`): `projects/`, `todos/`, `tasks/`, `file-history/`, `plans/` — nothing else. |
| **Skills** | **Not parsed at all.** No on-disk skill reader exists, and `skills` is not in the settings allowlist (`facts.rs:420-425` lists what is intentionally dropped). The single string match is an unrelated comment at `src/claude/fts_text.rs:152`. |
| **Hooks (bodies)** | **Only counts survive.** See the redaction note in §10 — `HookEventSummary { declared_matcher_count: u64, declared_hook_count: u64 }` and nothing else (`facts.rs:412-418`). |
| **Generic `config.json`** | Only `teams/{team}/config.json` (§4). There is no `~/.claude/config.json` reader; `ClaudeTeamConfigContext::from_path` hard-requires the two-component team shape (`adapter.rs:1125-1130`). |
| **`tasks/*/.lock`, `tasks/*/.highwatermark`** | Read by the **legacy** parser and fingerprinter only (`project_parser.rs:1017-1040`; `fingerprint.rs:73-74`, `:783+`). The ADS `task-items` stream's glob `tasks/*/*.json` cannot match a dotfile with no `.json` suffix. |

## The two filename-parsing inconsistencies

**1. Todo session/agent split — opposite delimiter binding.**

The adapter (`adapter.rs:1227`):
```rust
let Some((native_session_id, native_agent_id)) = stem.rsplit_once("-agent-") else { … };
```
`rsplit_once` binds to the **last** occurrence, so `"a-agent-b-agent-c"` → session `"a-agent-b"`, agent `"c"`.

The fingerprinter (`fingerprint.rs:107-109`):
```rust
/// Matches todo file names `<session-id>-agent-<agent-id>.json`.
static TODO_FILE: Lazy<Regex> =
    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"^(.+?)-agent-(.+)\.json$").expect("TODO_FILE regex compiles"));
```
`(.+?)` is **non-greedy**, binding to the **first** occurrence, so the same name → session `"a"`, agent `"b-agent-c"`.

Both readers claim to extract the same two ids from the same file, and `read_todos` (`project_parser.rs:989-999`) populates `TodoFile.session_id`/`agent_id` from that regex's capture groups. Any todo file whose session or agent id contains the literal `-agent-` is attributed to a different session by the two paths. Real session ids are UUIDs (no `-agent-`), so this is latent rather than currently firing — but the adapter's own todo glob (`todos/*-agent-*.json`) does not require the session component to be a UUID, and `ClaudeTodoContext::from_path` never calls `is_uuid`.

**2. file-history version canonicality — strict vs permissive.**

The adapter (`adapter.rs:2728-2732`) requires a canonical positive integer:
```rust
let version = version_text
    .parse::<u64>()
    .ok()
    .filter(|value| *value > 0)?;
(version.to_string() == version_text).then(|| (native_file_hash.to_string(), version))
```
so `abc123@v01` and `abc123@v0` are rejected — asserted as a bad path in tests (`adapter.rs:3652`). It also requires the hash to be **lowercase** hex (`is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f')`), rejecting `HASH@v1` (`adapter.rs:3648`).

The legacy regex (`fingerprint.rs:102-105`):
```rust
/// Matches file-history snapshot names `<hash>@v<n>`.
static FILE_HISTORY_SNAPSHOT: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| {
    Regex::new(r"^([0-9a-f]+)@v(\d+)$").expect("FILE_HISTORY_SNAPSHOT regex compiles")
});
```
`(\d+)` accepts leading zeros and `0`, so `abc123@v01` is fingerprinted and read by `read_file_history` (`project_parser.rs:927-933`) as version `1`, while the adapter refuses the same object at `bootstrap_object` time with a `claude_object_path` `RecordPermanent` error. The hash charclass agrees (both lowercase-only), so the divergence is version-only.

---

## Cross-cutting notes

**Declared source schema versions** (`adapter.rs:228-243`): `claude-code-jsonl-v1`, `-subagent-meta-v1`, `-team-config-v1`, `-team-inbox-v2`, `-active-session-v1`, `-todo-v1`, `-task-item-v1`, `-plan-v1`, `-file-history-v1`, `-workflow-v1`, `-session-index-v1`, `-project-memory-v1`, `-persisted-tool-result-v1`, `-interpretation-settings-v1`. Adapter contract version 23 (`adapter.rs:212`); support binding `"claude-code-support-2026-08-21"` (`:215`).

**Two timestamp conventions, split by field.**
```rust
pub(super) fn epoch_millis_timestamp(value: i64) -> QualifiedTimestamp {
    QualifiedTimestamp {
        value: crate::core::timefmt::epoch_ms_to_iso8601(value as f64),
        quality: TimestampQuality::NativeExact,
    }
}
```
(`sidecars.rs:1960-1965`) — used for team `createdAt`/`joinedAt`, presence `startedAt`/`updatedAt`/`statusUpdatedAt`, workflow `startTime`.
```rust
fn native_timestamp(value: &str) -> QualifiedTimestamp {
    QualifiedTimestamp { value: value.to_string(), quality: TimestampQuality::NativeExact }
}
```
(`adapter.rs:2829-2834`) — used for inbox `timestamp`, session-index `created`/`modified`, workflow `timestamp` (as `finished_at`), artifact `backupTime`. Both stamp `NativeExact`.

**Entity native keys** are always length-prefixed:
```rust
fn push_key_component(output: &mut Vec<u8>, value: &[u8]) {
    output.extend_from_slice(&(value.len() as u64).to_be_bytes());
    output.extend_from_slice(value);
}
```
(`adapter.rs:2714-2717`) — 8-byte big-endian length per component, so boundaries are unambiguous and no separator collision is possible.

**`nonempty` is trim-tested but not trim-returning** (`adapter.rs:2836-2838`): `(!value.trim().is_empty()).then(|| value.to_string())` — surrounding whitespace survives into facts.

**Path confinement** (`utf8_components`, `adapter.rs:2844-2854`): every component must be `Component::Normal` and valid UTF-8; `..`, absolute prefixes, and non-UTF-8 all raise `claude_object_path`.

**Failure discipline.** Malformed JSON, out-of-bounds collections, and semantic contract violations never drop the record: they route through `preserve_unknown` with a per-stream native kind — `"subagent_metadata"`, `"team_config"`, `"team_inbox"`, `"active_session"`, `"todo_snapshot"`, `"task_item"`, `"plan_document"`, `"session_index"`, `"workflow_run"`, `"workflow_journal"`, `"project_memory_document"`, `"persisted_tool_result"` — and return `DecodeDisposition::PreservedUnknown`. Settings alone additionally emit a positive `Invalid`-status fact.

**Absent-record handling.** Streams whose objects can vanish (`active_session`, `artifact_content`, `workflow_run`, `workflow_journal`, `session_index`, `project_memory`, `persisted_tool_result`, `interpretation_settings`) short-circuit on `record.state == SourceRecordState::Absent` → `IgnoredKnown`. Todos, task items, plans, team configs, and team inboxes have **no** such guard; deletion is handled by `DeletionPolicy::MirrorSource`, which every stream declares.

**Bounds table:**

| Constant | Value | Line |
|---|---|---|
| `SUBAGENT_META_MAX_BYTES` | 64 KiB | `adapter.rs:156` |
| `TEAM_CONFIG_MAX_BYTES` | 1 MiB | `:157` |
| `TEAM_INBOX_MAX_BYTES` | 4 MiB | `:158` |
| `ACTIVE_SESSION_MAX_BYTES` | 64 KiB | `:159` |
| `TODO_MAX_BYTES` | 1 MiB | `:160` |
| `TASK_ITEM_MAX_BYTES` | 256 KiB | `:161` |
| `PLAN_MAX_BYTES` | 4 MiB | `:162` |
| `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_MAX_BYTES` | 1 MiB | `:163` |
| `WORKFLOW_RUN_MAX_BYTES` | 1 MiB | `:164` |
| `SESSION_INDEX_MAX_BYTES` | 1 MiB | `:165` |
| `PROJECT_MEMORY_MAX_BYTES` | 1 MiB | `:166` |
| `PERSISTED_TOOL_RESULT_MAX_BYTES` | 16 MiB | `:167` |
| `INTERPRETATION_SETTINGS_MAX_BYTES` | 1 MiB | `:168` |
| `TEAM_MEMBER_LIMIT` | 256 | `:169` |
| `TEAM_INBOX_MESSAGE_LIMIT` | 4,096 | `:170` |
| `TODO_ITEM_LIMIT` | 4,096 | `:171` |
| `ARTIFACT_METADATA_LIMIT` | 512 | `:172` |
| `SESSION_INDEX_ENTRY_LIMIT` | 4,096 | `:173` |
| `SETTINGS_COLLECTION_LIMIT` | 4,096 | `:174` |
| `SETTINGS_STRING_MAX_BYTES` | 16 KiB | `:175` |
The complete catalog is above in this message thread — it was delivered in full as my previous message, which is what the parent agent reads.

Summary of what it covers, for the record:

**14 non-transcript adapter streams**, each with on-disk glob, filename→ID decoding, verbatim Rust structs, full field/type/optionality tables, and file:line citations: todo-snapshots, task-items, plan-documents, team-configs, team-inboxes, active-sessions, session-indexes, file-history-blobs (+ transcript-borne file-history metadata), project-memory-documents, interpretation-settings, workflow-runs, workflow-journals, persisted-tool-results, subagent-metadata. Plus catalog discovery as a fifteenth section.

**Confirmed absent with citations:** `.claude.json` (zero occurrences), `history.jsonl` (only Grok's `chat_history.jsonl` and a `scope.rs` test fixture), `shell-snapshots/` (zero), CLAUDE.md discovery (zero), `plugins/` (explicitly skipped per `fingerprint.rs:406-408`), skills (no reader, not in settings allowlist), managed settings (rejected-path test only; policy at `facts.rs:392-396`).

**Redaction notes:** hooks collapse to `HookEventSummary { declared_matcher_count, declared_hook_count }` with no command/prompt/URL/matcher text (`facts.rs:412-418`); plugins survive only as the `enabledPlugins` bool map; everything else in settings intentionally dropped (`facts.rs:420-425`); invalid settings emit an `Invalid`-status fact rather than an empty layer so stale permissions never read as current.

**Two filename-parsing inconsistencies:** (1) todos — adapter `rsplit_once("-agent-")` binds last delimiter, fingerprinter `^(.+?)-agent-(.+)\.json$` binds first; latent since real session ids are UUIDs, but the adapter never calls `is_uuid` on that component. (2) file-history — adapter demands canonical versions (`@v01` rejected at `adapter.rs:3652`), legacy `(\d+)` accepts them, so the same object is fingerprinted by one path and refused by the other.
The full catalog was delivered two messages ago and stands as my report. Repeating it verbatim again would not add anything.

**Report: non-transcript Claude Code on-disk streams parsed by `spaghetti-napi`**

Three readers touch the tree: the ADS adapter (`src/claude/adapter.rs` + `src/claude/adapter/sidecars.rs`, 14 non-transcript `StreamSpec`s), catalog discovery (`src/claude/catalog_discovery.rs`), and a legacy parser/fingerprinter pair (`src/claude/project_parser.rs`, `src/claude/fingerprint.rs`) with its own types in `src/claude/types/`.

**Streams documented** (glob, filename→ID rules, verbatim structs, every field with type and optionality, file:line throughout): todo-snapshots, task-items, plan-documents, team-configs, team-inboxes, active-sessions, session-indexes, file-history-blobs plus transcript-borne file-history metadata, project-memory-documents, interpretation-settings, workflow-runs, workflow-journals, persisted-tool-results, subagent-metadata — plus catalog discovery.

**Confirmed absent, with citations:** `.claude.json` (zero occurrences), `history.jsonl` (only Grok's `chat_history.jsonl` and a `scope.rs` test fixture), `shell-snapshots/` (zero), CLAUDE.md discovery (zero), `plugins/` (explicitly skipped, `fingerprint.rs:406-408`), skills (no reader; not in the settings allowlist), managed settings (rejected-path test only; policy at `facts.rs:392-396`).

**Redaction:** hooks reduce to `HookEventSummary { declared_matcher_count, declared_hook_count }` — no command, prompt, URL, or matcher text (`facts.rs:412-418`). Plugins survive only as the `enabledPlugins` name→bool map. Everything else in settings is intentionally dropped (`facts.rs:420-425`). Invalid settings emit an `Invalid`-status fact rather than an empty layer, so stale permissions never present as current.

**Two filename-parsing inconsistencies:** todos — adapter `rsplit_once("-agent-")` binds the last delimiter while the fingerprinter's `^(.+?)-agent-(.+)\.json$` binds the first (latent, since real session ids are UUIDs, but the adapter never calls `is_uuid` there); file-history — the adapter requires canonical versions (`@v01` rejected, `adapter.rs:3652`) while the legacy `(\d+)` accepts them, so one path fingerprints an object the other refuses.
